===== ./drizzle.config.ts =====

export default defineConfig({
  dialect: "postgresql",
  schema: "./server/database/schema.ts",
  out: "./server/database/migrations",
  dbCredentials: {
    url:
      process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "postgres://forma:forma@localhost:5432/forma",
  },
  strict: true,
  verbose: true,
});


===== ./docs/development/testing.md =====
# Testing

`npm test` runs Vitest unit tests for document validation/serialization/migrations, parser and editor adapter, calculation graph and engineering regression, commands/history, local recovery, application-service authorization, units, and constants.

Integration tests use a separate PostgreSQL database and never create/drop the configured database. Create it, apply migrations, then run:

```sh
TEST_DATABASE_URL=postgres://forma:forma-local-only@localhost:5432/forma_test npm run test:integration
```

Playwright tests create isolated accounts and worksheets in a separate database. Create and migrate it once:

```sh
docker compose exec -T database createdb -U forma forma_e2e
DATABASE_URL=postgres://forma:forma-local-only@localhost:5432/forma_e2e npm run db:migrate
```

Run the browser suite against that database:

```sh
DATABASE_URL=postgres://forma:forma-local-only@localhost:5432/forma_e2e \
BETTER_AUTH_URL=http://127.0.0.1:3001 \
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://127.0.0.1:3001 \
npm run test:e2e
```

The E2E launcher builds the current source, prepares Next standalone assets, and runs an isolated server on port 3001. The suite covers registration, calculation editing, save/reopen, sharing, editor/viewer permissions, and stale revision conflicts. `npm run typecheck` and `npm run build` are required gates. CI uses its own ephemeral PostgreSQL service. CI configuration is in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.


===== ./docs/development/contribution-guide.md =====
# Contribution Guide

- Keep `domain/**` independent of React, Next.js, and database packages.
- Treat documents and API payloads as untrusted; validate them at boundaries.
- Add explicit migrations for document/database schema changes.
- Add focused tests with expected values independently checked for engineering calculations.
- Keep math editor vendor types and serialization behind `features/editor/math`.
- Do not add executable user code or render imported HTML as trusted content.
- Run typecheck, unit tests, database integration tests when repository behavior changes, and the production build before proposing changes.
- Update the relevant ADR when changing a significant architecture decision.


===== ./docs/development/local-setup.md =====
# Local Setup

1. Install Docker Engine and Compose.
2. Run `docker compose up --build` from the repository root.
3. Open http://localhost:3000 and create an account.
4. Stop with Ctrl+C; named PostgreSQL volumes persist. `docker compose down` stops containers but retains data. Do not use `down -v` unless intentionally deleting local databases.

The Compose stack uses a development-only PostgreSQL password and auth secret. To change the auth secret, set `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` in the shell before Compose startup. Production secrets must be independently generated and managed.

Host-based commands need the same variables from `.env.example`. `DATABASE_URL` must point to a database with current migrations. Integration tests use a different database configured with `TEST_DATABASE_URL`.

Playwright uses `forma_e2e`, not the normal `forma` development database. Provision and migrate it using [the testing guide](testing.md) before running browser tests.

===== ./docs/architecture/units-and-constants.md =====
# Units and Constants

The initial `domain/units` subset models SI base dimensions, common engineering units (mm, kN, MPa, GPa, N, J, W), radians/degrees, and absolute/delta Celsius/Kelvin temperatures. Quantities store SI magnitude, a seven-axis dimension vector, temperature kind, and display unit. Operations enforce dimensional compatibility and treat absolute temperatures separately from differences.

`domain/constants` stores stable IDs, symbols, values, units, source names, and source versions. Values are data, not evaluator globals.

Library evaluation:

- Math.js 15.3.0 (Apache-2.0) includes TypeScript declarations and unit-aware arithmetic, but is a broad math/expression platform with a separate value/type model. Adopting its parser/evaluator would bypass Forma's restricted AST/grammar unless carefully wrapped.
- `js-quantities` 1.8.0 (MIT) targets quantities and conversions but does not publish a TypeScript `types`/`typings` field in npm metadata; a maintained local type boundary would be required.
- `convert-units` 2.3.4 (MIT) focuses on conversion tables and does not provide the dimensional algebra needed for engineering expression evaluation.

For this small, tested foundation, the typed SI implementation is retained rather than adding a broad evaluator or a conversion-only dependency. Re-evaluate Math.js's unit primitives or another typed quantity library when integrating quantities into expression evaluation; compare dimension behavior, affine temperatures, aliases, license, bundle/server footprint, and safe AST integration before adopting.

The current unit subsystem is not yet parsed from MathLive input, evaluated alongside worksheet variables, displayed in results, or used to validate dimensions in a full calculation graph.


===== ./docs/architecture/deployment-architecture.md =====
# Deployment Architecture

The application is a Next.js Node.js server with PostgreSQL. `compose.yaml` starts both for local development, persists database files in a named volume, waits for PostgreSQL health, and applies migrations before `next dev`. The production `Dockerfile` is multi-stage, uses Next standalone output, and runs as a non-root user.

Production startup does not migrate implicitly. Build and deploy the image, provide environment configuration/secrets, run `npm run db:migrate` as an explicit release step, then start `node server.js`. `/api/health/live` reports process availability; `/api/health/ready` checks PostgreSQL.

No queue, worker, Redis, or external service is deployed. Calculation remains in-process. Backups and restores are PostgreSQL operations; application-container storage is not authoritative.


===== ./docs/architecture/security-architecture.md =====
# Security Architecture

Trust boundaries:

- Browser input, local drafts, and imported JSON are untrusted. Documents are size-bounded and Zod-validated at serialization/API/database boundaries.
- Worksheet text is rendered as React text/input content, not trusted HTML. Math is parsed through an allowlisted grammar; no `eval`, `Function`, plugins, or document-supplied JavaScript is executed.
- Better Auth owns password hashing and database-backed session management. Server routes and page entry check sessions; every worksheet read/write checks owner/editor/viewer access in application/repository paths.
- SQL is emitted through parameterized Drizzle operations. Database URLs and auth secrets are server-only environment variables.

Implemented headers include CSP, `X-Content-Type-Options`, `X-Frame-Options`, referrer/permissions policy, and HSTS. CSP is self-hosted and restrictive in origin, but uses `unsafe-inline` for Next/MathLive compatibility; production nonce-based scripts/styles are a hardening follow-up. Better Auth uses trusted origins and secure framework-managed cookies; deployment must use HTTPS and a strong secret.

Request bodies are capped at about 2.1 MB; document limits are enforced separately. Writes have an in-process rate limiter and request IDs. That limiter is not shared across replicas and must be complemented by an ingress/provider limit in a multi-instance deployment. Structured logs omit document content, passwords, cookies, and tokens.

Email verification, account recovery policy, external abuse controls, distributed rate limiting, uploaded assets, and automated dependency scanning in CI remain incomplete. The local Compose password/auth secret are development-only and must never be used in production.


===== ./docs/architecture/worksheet-document-model.md =====
# Worksheet Document Model

The authoritative document is a versioned JSON object validated with Zod. Version 1 contains an ID, title, page/grid settings, and a discriminated math/text region list. Region IDs are stable; calculation results, selection, cursor, drag state, and zoom are not serialized.

Coordinates use millimeters on a configurable page. The current default is A4 (210 × 297 mm). The renderer maps document coordinates into CSS percentages; browser pixels are never persisted. Region `zIndex` orders overlaps, and overlap is allowed. Regions currently size to content; the optional size field is reserved for explicit sizing. The current page is finite and clipped; infinite canvas and multiple pages are deferred.

Serialization is JSON and independent of React/PostgreSQL. Runtime validation limits the serialized payload to 2 MB, 2,000 regions, 20,000 characters per text region, coordinate/page bounds, and expression nesting depth. The legacy prototype's percentage coordinates migrate to millimeters and its math text is parsed into AST nodes.

`serializeWorksheet` and `deserializeWorksheet` are covered by round-trip, validation, size-limit, depth-limit, and legacy migration tests. New schema versions require explicit migrations; only the prototype-to-v1 migration exists today.


===== ./docs/architecture/calculation-engine.md =====
# Calculation Engine

The calculation package is independent of React and PostgreSQL. It parses prototype-compatible input into AST nodes, extracts identifiers, constructs region dependencies, topologically evaluates assignments independent of spatial order, identifies cycles, and returns typed region statuses/errors.

Duplicate definitions are rejected for every duplicate region; references to a duplicate are also errors. Undefined identifiers are explicit errors. Empty math regions are pending. Re-definitions are not sequential assignments. Supported results are finite real scalars; division by zero, non-finite operations, unsupported constructs, and dependency failures have structured codes.

`CalculationSession` compares math-region ASTs, rebuilds the graph, invalidates the changed node and old/new transitive dependents, and reuses unaffected results. Tests assert both expected engineering values and the recalculated region set. It is synchronous and in-process today. Timeouts, cancellation, progress, and a separate worker are deferred.

The SI quantity subsystem is separately tested, but quantities are not yet connected to AST evaluation, dependency results, or the UI. Dimensional mismatch is therefore not yet an evaluator result.


===== ./docs/architecture/database-model.md =====
# Database Model

Drizzle ORM with the `postgres` driver is used against PostgreSQL. The domain model does not import either. The initial schema is generated and applied through checked-in Drizzle migrations.

Tables: Better Auth `user`, `session`, `account`, and `verification`; `worksheets` with owner, title, schema version, revision, JSONB content, timestamps, and soft deletion; `worksheet_revisions` with immutable JSONB snapshots and request IDs; `worksheet_permissions` with editor/viewer levels; and `audit_events`.

Worksheet content stays as JSONB. Ownership, access, revision, and timestamps are relational. Saves lock the worksheet row, verify permission and expected revision, update current content, insert a revision, and write an audit event in one transaction. `(worksheet_id, request_id)` supports idempotent retries. A stale base revision returns a conflict; client content is retained.

Repository integration tests use a separate `TEST_DATABASE_URL` and exercise migrations, access, revisions, retry behavior, conflicts, and rollback. Production migrations are an explicit deployment step.


===== ./docs/architecture/module-boundaries.md =====
# Module Boundaries

| Module                | Owns                                                                 | Must not depend on         |
| --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- |
| `domain/worksheet`    | Document model, runtime validation, serialization                    | React, Next.js, PostgreSQL |
| `domain/expressions`  | AST and restricted parser                                            | UI and persistence         |
| `domain/calculation`  | Dependencies, numerical evaluation, structured results               | React, database clients    |
| `domain/units`        | Dimensions, units, quantity operations                               | UI and database            |
| `features/editor`     | Document commands, history, editor state, MathLive adapter           | PostgreSQL                 |
| `features/worksheets` | Workspace workflows and client persistence integration               | Database client            |
| `server/services`     | Authorization-aware worksheet use cases                              | UI components              |
| `server/repositories` | PostgreSQL persistence implementation                                | UI components              |
| `app/api`             | Request parsing, auth boundary, service invocation, response mapping | Domain algorithms          |

Database access is obtained through the server-only client and `WorksheetRepository`. Auth and worksheet services are called by route handlers. Workers are deferred; the calculation session is a synchronous domain capability that can later be wrapped by a worker contract.

Some React workflow logic remains in `features/worksheets/components/worksheet-workspace.tsx`; splitting it further is follow-up work. There is no dependency-boundary lint rule yet.


===== ./docs/architecture/overview.md =====
# Architecture Overview

Forma is a modular monolith: one Next.js App Router application, one PostgreSQL database, and explicit internal boundaries. No queue or remote calculation service is introduced.

Implemented modules:

- `domain/worksheet`: framework-independent document types, schema validation, serialization, and prototype migration.
- `domain/expressions`: structured AST and restricted prototype-syntax parser.
- `domain/calculation`: evaluator, graph, structured errors, and an incremental in-process session.
- `domain/units` and `domain/constants`: tested SI quantity operations and source-versioned constants; not yet connected to worksheet evaluation.
- `features/editor`, `features/regions`, `features/worksheets`, `features/auth`: spatial editor, editor state/history, region rendering, persistence UI, and auth client.
- `server/auth`, `server/api`, `server/database`, `server/repositories`, `server/services`: identity, request handling, PostgreSQL, repository, and application-service boundaries.

The document and calculation modules do not import React, Next.js, or database clients. The database repository validates JSONB content before returning it. APIs authenticate, validate, invoke services, and serialize structured outcomes.

Implemented state is documented as such; planned capabilities are not presented as available features. See [module boundaries](module-boundaries.md), [worksheet document](worksheet-document-model.md), [expression model](expression-model.md), [units and constants](units-and-constants.md), and the [ADRs](../adr/0001-modular-monolith.md).


===== ./docs/architecture/expression-model.md =====
# Expression Model

Forma stores supported math as a discriminated AST: numeric literals, identifiers, constants, unary/binary operations, fractions, roots, powers, subscripts, functions, and quantity nodes. Assignments are a separate statement node. The supported text parser uses an explicit allowlisted grammar; it never evaluates JavaScript.

MathLive 0.111.4 (MIT) is integrated behind `MathEditorAdapter`. It provides direct structured editing, cursor movement, fractions, scripts, symbol shortcuts, selection, and virtual keyboard capabilities. The adapter consumes MathLive AsciiMath output and converts the supported subset into Forma AST. MathLive's own serialization is not authoritative, and its LaTeX is not required from students.

The adapter deliberately rejects unsupported editor output instead of executing or persisting opaque markup. Matrices, vectors, comparisons, arbitrary function definitions, and MathLive constructs outside the current grammar do not yet have complete AST/evaluator support. Quantity AST nodes validate and render, but are not parsed from the current input grammar or evaluated by the worksheet engine.

This is a credible initial editor integration, not the complete SMath/Mathcad interaction model. Browser tests should continue to cover fraction insertion, keyboard navigation, shortcuts, and import/export as those behaviors are expanded.


===== ./docs/operations/configuration.md =====
# Configuration

| Variable              | Required                      | Purpose                                         |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| `DATABASE_URL`        | Yes                           | Server-only PostgreSQL connection string        |
| `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET`  | Yes outside local development | Session/signing secret; never expose to browser |
| `BETTER_AUTH_URL`     | Yes in deployment             | Canonical trusted auth origin                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` | Yes for client auth           | Public application origin; contains no secret   |
| `DATABASE_POOL_MAX`   | No                            | PostgreSQL pool size, default 10                |
| `POSTGRES_PORT`       | No, Compose only              | Local host port, default 5432                   |

`.env.example` contains local placeholders only. The Compose password and fallback auth secret are development-only. Do not use `.env.example` as a production secret source.


===== ./docs/operations/backup-restore.md =====
# Backup and Restore

Use PostgreSQL-native backups. For local Compose, a logical backup can be made with:

```sh
docker compose exec -T database pg_dump -U forma -Fc forma > forma.dump
```

Restore into a separately created target database after verifying the backup and target identity:

```sh
docker compose exec -T database pg_restore -U forma -d forma_restore --clean --if-exists < forma.dump
```

Production backup encryption, retention, offsite replication, and restore-time objectives depend on the hosting provider and are not configured by this repository. Do not put database dumps in the source tree or container image.


===== ./docs/operations/deployment.md =====
# Deployment

Build the production image with `docker build -t forma:release .`. Configure `DATABASE_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET`, and `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` through the deployment platform's secret/configuration facility. Use HTTPS at the public boundary and a secret of at least 32 random bytes.

Before starting the release, run the migration step once with the same image and database configuration: `npm run db:migrate`. Then start the container. Migrations are not run automatically by the production entrypoint. Verify `/api/health/live` and `/api/health/ready` after rollout.

Persistent user data lives in PostgreSQL, not the app container. Configure database backups, restore drills, TLS/network policy, ingress rate limits, and centralized structured log collection. The current in-process API rate limiter is not sufficient by itself for multiple replicas.


===== ./docs/adr/0004-database-and-persistence.md =====
# ADR 0004: PostgreSQL, Drizzle, and JSONB

## Context

Forma needs authoritative ownership, sharing, revisions, and optimistic concurrency while retaining a versioned document format.

## Decision

Use PostgreSQL, Drizzle ORM, the `postgres` driver, and checked-in Drizzle migrations. Store document JSONB in `worksheets`; keep identity, access, revision numbers, timestamps, and audit metadata relational.

## Alternatives considered

Browser-only persistence, manually managed SQL without migrations, and normalizing each region into tables.

## Consequences

Transactions can update current content and revision history atomically; JSONB preserves domain serialization.

## Known limitations

The current integration suite requires an explicitly provisioned test database. Collaboration/merge and revision pruning are deferred.


===== ./docs/adr/0007-units-library.md =====
# ADR 0007: Initial SI Quantity Boundary

## Context

Engineering calculations need typed quantities, dimensional checks, conversion, and distinct absolute/relative temperature semantics. Full unit coverage is not part of this iteration.

## Decision

Implement a small framework-independent SI subset in `domain/units`, with explicit dimension vectors, affine temperature kinds, conversion operations, and tests. Store physical constants as sourced, versioned data. Do not feed unit strings or arbitrary expressions to a general-purpose evaluator.

## Alternatives considered

- Math.js 15.3.0 (Apache-2.0) has bundled TypeScript types and unit arithmetic but also provides a broad expression/evaluation model that would need a strict adapter to preserve Forma's grammar.
- `js-quantities` 1.8.0 (MIT) is quantity-focused but has no `types`/`typings` field in npm metadata.
- `convert-units` 2.3.4 (MIT) handles conversions but not dimensional algebra.

## Consequences

The implemented SI surface is small, explicit, and independently testable. It avoids a new runtime dependency during initial AST integration.

## Known limitations

Units are not parsed from math regions or connected to calculation results. Reassess a typed quantity library when expression support is integrated; the hand-maintained registry is not intended to grow into a comprehensive unit database.


===== ./docs/adr/0003-mathematical-editor.md =====
# ADR 0003: MathLive Behind an Internal Adapter

## Context

The required experience is direct editing of nested mathematical structures, not a LaTeX text field.

## Decision

Use MathLive 0.111.4 (MIT), isolated behind `MathEditorAdapter`. MathLive supplies in-place editing, navigation, shortcuts, fraction/root/script structures, selection, and virtual keyboard. Forma converts supported AsciiMath output to its own AST and persists that AST.

## Alternatives considered

Rendering-only libraries, a plain text/LaTeX editor, and a custom DOM-based structured editor. Rendering-only does not satisfy direct editing; a complete custom editor is too large to build safely in this phase.

## Consequences

Students do not need to type LaTeX. Vendor APIs and serialization do not enter the worksheet model. Licensing is MIT.

## Known limitations

Only the Forma numeric grammar is accepted by the adapter. Matrix/vector input, full MathJSON extraction, and complete SMath-like region/cursor workflows require further work. Rendering/editor behavior must be covered by browser regression tests.


===== ./docs/adr/0006-calculation-execution.md =====
# ADR 0006: Restricted In-Process Calculation

## Context

Basic worksheets need responsive, safe numerical calculation without allowing arbitrary code execution or introducing a remote service.

## Decision

Use a framework-independent AST evaluator with explicit grammar, dependency graph, cycle detection, structured results, and an incremental `CalculationSession`. Keep execution in-process behind a separate calculation API.

## Alternatives considered

JavaScript `eval`, sequential region evaluation, a remote calculation microservice, and a queue-backed worker.

## Consequences

Calculations are deterministic, testable, and local. The domain can later be called from a worker without coupling to UI/database modules.

## Known limitations

Current values are real scalars. Units exist as a tested independent domain subsystem but are not integrated into expression evaluation. Timeouts/cancellation/workload isolation are deferred until expensive workloads exist.


===== ./docs/adr/0002-document-format.md =====
# ADR 0002: Versioned JSON Document

## Context

Worksheets are spatial documents with stable regions and must survive UI/database changes.

## Decision

Use a typed, versioned JSON document validated with Zod. Coordinates are page millimeters. Region collections and ASTs are stored in PostgreSQL JSONB. Derived calculation results and transient editor state are excluded.

## Alternatives considered

HTML/DOM serialization, screenshots, normalized region tables, and unversioned localStorage objects.

## Consequences

Documents can be validated, migrated, exported, and round-tripped independently of React/PostgreSQL. Schema changes require explicit migration code.

## Known limitations

Only v1 plus a prototype-format migration exists. The current layout is one finite A4 page; pages/infinite canvas remain open product decisions.


===== ./docs/adr/0001-modular-monolith.md =====
# ADR 0001: Modular Monolith

## Context

Forma needs clear domain/server/editor boundaries without premature distributed operations.

## Decision

Use one Next.js Node.js application and PostgreSQL database. Domain, feature, server, and repository modules expose explicit TypeScript contracts. No queue or service split is introduced.

## Alternatives considered

Microservices, a separate calculation service, and direct database access from routes or UI.

## Consequences

Modules can be tested independently and selected workloads can later move behind a worker contract. Initial scaling is process/application scaling. There is no independent worker deployment today.

## Known limitations

Some feature orchestration is still in a large workspace component; dependency rules are documented but not mechanically linted.


===== ./docs/adr/0005-authentication.md =====
# ADR 0005: Better Auth

## Context

Worksheet APIs require established password/session handling and server-side authorization.

## Decision

Use Better Auth with its Drizzle PostgreSQL adapter and email/password flow. Sessions are database-backed, short-lived, and checked by server page/API boundaries. Ownership and editor/viewer permission are separate authorization checks.

## Alternatives considered

Custom password/session code, browser-only checks, and OAuth-only integration requiring external provider credentials.

## Consequences

Forma avoids implementing password hashing or session cryptography and stores auth tables in the same migration-managed database.

## Known limitations

Email verification and account recovery workflows are not configured. Deployment requires a generated strong secret, trusted HTTPS origin, and operational account-abuse controls.


===== ./server/services/worksheet-service.ts =====
import { validateWorksheetDocument } from "../../domain/worksheet/validation";
import type {
  WorksheetRepository,
  WorksheetRecord,
} from "../repositories/worksheet-repository";

export class WorksheetApplicationError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: 400 | 401 | 403 | 404 | 409,
    readonly code:
      | "invalid_document"
      | "unauthenticated"
      | "forbidden"
      | "not_found"
      | "revision_conflict"
      | "idempotency_conflict",
    message: string,
    readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export class WorksheetService {
  constructor(private readonly repository: WorksheetRepository) {}

  list(userId: string): Promise<WorksheetRecord[]> {
    return this.repository.listAccessible(userId);
  }

  async get(worksheetId: string, userId: string): Promise<WorksheetRecord> {
    const record = await this.repository.findAccessible(worksheetId, userId);
    if (!record)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        404,
        "not_found",
        "Worksheet not found",
      );
    return record;
  }

  async create(userId: string, input: unknown): Promise<WorksheetRecord> {
    const document = this.validate(input);
    return this.repository.create(userId, document);
  }

  async save(
    worksheetId: string,
    userId: string,
    expectedRevision: number,
    requestId: string,
    input: unknown,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord> {
    const current = await this.get(worksheetId, userId);
    if (current.permission === "viewer") {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        403,
        "forbidden",
        "Viewer access cannot modify worksheets",
      );
    }
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 1) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "A valid base revision is required",
      );
    }
    if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,128}$/.test(requestId)) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "A valid request ID is required",
      );
    }
    const document = this.validate(input);
    if (document.id !== worksheetId) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "Document ID does not match the worksheet route",
      );
    }

    const outcome = await this.repository.save({
      worksheetId,
      actorId: userId,
      expectedRevision,
      requestId,
      document,
    });
    if (outcome.kind === "saved") return outcome.record;
    if (outcome.kind === "conflict") {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        409,
        "revision_conflict",
        "Worksheet changed since this draft was loaded",
        {
          currentRevision: outcome.record.revision,
        },
      );
    }
    if (outcome.kind === "idempotency-conflict") {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        409,
        "idempotency_conflict",
        "Request ID was already used for a different document",
      );
    }
    if (outcome.kind === "forbidden")
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        403,
        "forbidden",
        "Worksheet access does not allow editing",
      );
    throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
      404,
      "not_found",
      "Worksheet not found",
    );
  }

  async delete(worksheetId: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const record = await this.get(worksheetId, userId);
    if (record.permission !== "owner")
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        403,
        "forbidden",
        "Only the owner can delete a worksheet",
      );
    if (!(await this.repository.softDelete(worksheetId, userId))) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        404,
        "not_found",
        "Worksheet not found",
      );
    }
  }

  async grantPermission(
    worksheetId: string,
    actorId: string,
    targetUserId: string,
    permission: "editor" | "viewer",
  ): Promise<void> {
    const record = await this.get(worksheetId, actorId);
    if (record.permission !== "owner")
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        403,
        "forbidden",
        "Only the owner can share a worksheet",
      );
    if (
      !targetUserId ||
      targetUserId.length > 128 ||
      targetUserId === actorId
    ) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "Invalid sharing principal",
      );
    }
    await this.repository.grantPermission(
      worksheetId,
      targetUserId,
      permission,
    );
  }

  async revisions(worksheetId: string, userId: string) {
    await this.get(worksheetId, userId);
    return this.repository.listRevisions(worksheetId);
  }

  private validate(input: unknown) {
    try {
      return validateWorksheetDocument(input);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Invalid worksheet document",
      );
    }
  }
}


===== ./server/services/container.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { getDatabase } from "../database/client";
import { DrizzleWorksheetRepository } from "../repositories/drizzle-worksheet-repository";
import { WorksheetService } from "./worksheet-service";

let worksheetService: WorksheetService | undefined;

export function getWorksheetService(): WorksheetService {
  worksheetService ??= new WorksheetService(
    new DrizzleWorksheetRepository(getDatabase()),
  );
  return worksheetService;
}


===== ./server/services/worksheet-service.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../domain/expressions/parser";
import {
  DEFAULT_PAGE,
  type WorksheetDocument,
} from "../../domain/worksheet/model";
import type {
  SaveOutcome,
  WorksheetPermission,
  WorksheetRecord,
  WorksheetRepository,
} from "../repositories/worksheet-repository";
import {
  WorksheetApplicationError,
  WorksheetService,
} from "./worksheet-service";

const document: WorksheetDocument = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "doc-1",
  title: "Torque",
  settings: { page: DEFAULT_PAGE, grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 } },
  regions: [
    {
      id: "formula",
      type: "math",
      content: parseMathStatement("torque := force * arm"),
      position: { x: 10, y: 20 },
      zIndex: 0,
    },
  ],
};

class TestRepository implements WorksheetRepository {
  permission: WorksheetPermission = "owner";
  current: WorksheetRecord = {
    document,
    ownerId: "owner",
    permission: "owner",
    revision: 1,
    updatedAt: new Date(0),
  };
  outcome: SaveOutcome = {
    kind: "saved",
    record: { ...this.current, revision: 2 },
    idempotent: false,
  };
  async create(): Promise<WorksheetRecord> {
    return this.current;
  }
  async listAccessible(): Promise<WorksheetRecord[]> {
    return [this.current];
  }
  async findAccessible(
    _id: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord | null> {
    return userId === "owner" || userId === "editor" || userId === "viewer"
      ? { ...this.current, permission: this.permission }
      : null;
  }
  async save(): Promise<SaveOutcome> {
    return this.outcome;
  }
  async softDelete(): Promise<boolean> {
    return true;
  }
  async grantPermission(): Promise<void> {}
  async listRevisions() {
    return [];
  }
}

describe("worksheet application service", () => {
  it("rejects viewers before attempting document persistence", async () => {
    const repository = new TestRepository();
    repository.permission = "viewer";
    const service = new WorksheetService(repository);
    await expect(
      service.save("doc-1", "viewer", 1, "save-1", document),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "forbidden" });
  });

  it("surfaces optimistic concurrency conflicts without replacing the client's document", async () => {
    const repository = new TestRepository();
    repository.outcome = {
      kind: "conflict",
      record: { ...repository.current, revision: 3 },
    };
    const service = new WorksheetService(repository);
    try {
      await service.save("doc-1", "owner", 1, "save-2", document);
      throw new Error("Expected revision conflict");
    } catch (error) {
      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(WorksheetApplicationError);
      expect(error).toMatchObject({
        status: 409,
        code: "revision_conflict",
        details: { currentRevision: 3 },
      });
    }
  });

  it("validates incoming documents and binds the document ID to the route", async () => {
    const service = new WorksheetService(new TestRepository());
    await expect(
      service.create("owner", { title: "unvalidated" }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400, code: "invalid_document" });
    await expect(
      service.save("other-id", "owner", 1, "save-3", document),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 400, code: "invalid_document" });
  });
});


===== ./server/database/schema.ts =====
import { relations } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  index,
  integer,
  jsonb,
  pgEnum,
  pgTable,
  primaryKey,
  text,
  timestamp,
  uniqueIndex,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

const createdAt = () =>
  timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow();
const updatedAt = () =>
  timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow();

export const users = pgTable("user", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean("email_verified").notNull().default(false),
  image: text("image"),
  createdAt: createdAt(),
  updatedAt: updatedAt(),
});

export const sessions = pgTable(
  "session",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    token: text("token").notNull().unique(),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
    ipAddress: text("ip_address"),
    userAgent: text("user_agent"),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (table) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)],
);

export const accounts = pgTable(
  "account",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    accountId: text("account_id").notNull(),
    providerId: text("provider_id").notNull(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    accessToken: text("access_token"),
    refreshToken: text("refresh_token"),
    idToken: text("id_token"),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("access_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("refresh_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    scope: text("scope"),
    password: text("password"),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
  },
  (table) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(table.userId)],
);

export const verifications = pgTable(
  "verification",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    identifier: text("identifier").notNull(),
    value: text("value").notNull(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
  },
  (table) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(table.identifier)],
);

export const worksheetPermission = pgEnum("worksheet_permission", [
  "editor",
  "viewer",
]);

export const worksheets = pgTable(
  "worksheets",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    ownerId: text("owner_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    title: text("title").notNull(),
    schemaVersion: integer("schema_version").notNull(),
    revision: integer("revision").notNull().default(1),
    content: jsonb("content").$type<unknown>().notNull(),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
    deletedAt: timestamp("deleted_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("worksheets_owner_updated_idx").on(table.ownerId, table.updatedAt),
  ],
);

export const worksheetRevisions = pgTable(
  "worksheet_revisions",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    worksheetId: text("worksheet_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => worksheets.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    revisionNumber: integer("revision_number").notNull(),
    content: jsonb("content").$type<unknown>().notNull(),
    schemaVersion: integer("schema_version").notNull(),
    createdBy: text("created_by")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "restrict" }),
    requestId: text("request_id"),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
  },
  (table) => [
    uniqueIndex("worksheet_revisions_number_uidx").on(
      table.worksheetId,
      table.revisionNumber,
    ),
    uniqueIndex("worksheet_revisions_request_uidx").on(
      table.worksheetId,
      table.requestId,
    ),
    index("worksheet_revisions_created_idx").on(
      table.worksheetId,
      table.createdAt,
    ),
  ],
);

export const worksheetPermissions = pgTable(
  "worksheet_permissions",
  {
    worksheetId: text("worksheet_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => worksheets.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    permission: worksheetPermission("permission").notNull(),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
  },
  (table) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [table.worksheetId, table.userId] }),
    index("worksheet_permissions_user_idx").on(table.userId),
  ],
);

export const auditEvents = pgTable(
  "audit_events",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    actorId: text("actor_id").references(() => users.id, {
      onDelete: "set null",
    }),
    resourceType: text("resource_type").notNull(),
    resourceId: text("resource_id").notNull(),
    action: text("action").notNull(),
    metadata: jsonb("metadata")
      .$type<Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>>()
      .notNull()
      .default({}),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
  },
  (table) => [
    index("audit_resource_created_idx").on(
      table.resourceType,
      table.resourceId,
      table.createdAt,
    ),
  ],
);

export const worksheetRelations = relations(worksheets, ({ many, one }) => ({
  owner: one(users, { fields: [worksheets.ownerId], references: [users.id] }),
  revisions: many(worksheetRevisions),
  permissions: many(worksheetPermissions),
}));

export const worksheetRevisionRelations = relations(
  worksheetRevisions,
  ({ one }) => ({
    worksheet: one(worksheets, {
      fields: [worksheetRevisions.worksheetId],
      references: [worksheets.id],
    }),
    author: one(users, {
      fields: [worksheetRevisions.createdBy],
      references: [users.id],
    }),
  }),
);

export const worksheetPermissionRelations = relations(
  worksheetPermissions,
  ({ one }) => ({
    worksheet: one(worksheets, {
      fields: [worksheetPermissions.worksheetId],
      references: [worksheets.id],
    }),
    user: one(users, {
      fields: [worksheetPermissions.userId],
      references: [users.id],
    }),
  }),
);

export const schema = {
  users,
  sessions,
  accounts,
  verifications,
  worksheets,
  worksheetRevisions,
  worksheetPermissions,
  auditEvents,
};

===== ./server/database/client.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";
import postgres from "postgres";
import { schema } from "./schema";

export type Database = PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>;

let database: Database | undefined;
let client: ReturnType<typeof postgres> | undefined;

export function getDatabase(): Database {
  if (database) return database;
  const connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
  if (!connectionString)
    throw new Error(
      "DATABASE_URL must be configured for server database access",
    );
  client = postgres(connectionString, {
    max: Number(process.env.DATABASE_POOL_MAX ?? 10),
    prepare: false,
  });
  database = drizzle(client, { schema });
  return database;
}

export async function closeDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  if (client) await client.end({ timeout: 5 });
  client = undefined;
  database = undefined;
}


===== ./server/auth/auth.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter";
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { getDatabase } from "../database/client";
import { accounts, sessions, users, verifications } from "../database/schema";

const developmentSecret =
  "forma-local-development-secret-do-not-use-in-production";

let authInstance: ReturnType<typeof createAuth> | undefined;

export function getAuth() {
  authInstance ??= createAuth();
  return authInstance;
}

function createAuth() {
  const configuredSecret = process.env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET;
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" && !configuredSecret) {
    throw new Error("BETTER_AUTH_SECRET must be set in production");
  }
  const baseURL = process.env.BETTER_AUTH_URL ?? "http://localhost:3000";

  return betterAuth({
    database: drizzleAdapter(getDatabase(), {
      provider: "pg",
      schema: {
        user: users,
        session: sessions,
        account: accounts,
        verification: verifications,
      },
    }),
    secret: configuredSecret ?? developmentSecret,
    baseURL,
    trustedOrigins: [baseURL],
    rateLimit: {
      customRules: {
        "/sign-up/email": { window: 60, max: 10 },
      },
    },
    emailAndPassword: { enabled: true },
    session: { expiresIn: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7, updateAge: 60 * 60 * 24 },
  });
}


===== ./server/auth/session.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { getAuth } from "./auth";

export async function currentSession() {
  return getAuth().api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
}

export async function sessionFromRequest(request: Request) {
  return getAuth().api.getSession({ headers: request.headers });
}


===== ./server/api/http.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { WorksheetApplicationError } from "../services/worksheet-service";
import { logServerError } from "../observability/logger";

export class RequestError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: 400 | 413,
    readonly code: "invalid_json" | "request_too_large",
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export function requestIdFor(request: Request): string {
  const supplied = request.headers.get("x-request-id");
  return supplied && /^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,128}$/.test(supplied)
    ? supplied
    : crypto.randomUUID();
}

export async function readJsonBody(
  request: Request,
  maxBytes = 2_100_000,
): Promise<unknown> {
  const contentLength = Number(request.headers.get("content-length"));
  if (Number.isFinite(contentLength) && contentLength > maxBytes) {
    throw new RequestError(
      413,
      "request_too_large",
      "Request body exceeds the allowed size",
    );
  }
  if (!request.body)
    throw new RequestError(
      400,
      "invalid_json",
      "A JSON request body is required",
    );

  const reader = request.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  while (true) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    total += value.byteLength;
    if (total > maxBytes) {
      await reader.cancel();
      throw new RequestError(
        413,
        "request_too_large",
        "Request body exceeds the allowed size",
      );
    }
    chunks.push(value);
  }

  const body = new Uint8Array(total);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    body.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.byteLength;
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(body),
    ) as unknown;
  } catch {
    throw new RequestError(
      400,
      "invalid_json",
      "Request body must be valid JSON",
    );
  }
}

export function errorResponse(error: unknown, requestId: string): NextResponse {
  if (error instanceof WorksheetApplicationError) {
    const { status, code, message, details } = error;
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: { code, message, details, requestId } },
      { status, headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  }
  if (error instanceof RequestError) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: { code: error.code, message: error.message, requestId } },
      { status: error.status, headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  }
  logServerError("api.request_failed", requestId, error);
  return NextResponse.json(
    {
      error: {
        code: "internal_error",
        message: "The request could not be completed",
        requestId,
      },
    },
    {
      status: 500,
      headers: responseHeaders(requestId),
    },
  );
}

export function responseHeaders(requestId: string): HeadersInit {
  return { "Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Request-Id": requestId };
}

export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}


===== ./server/api/rate-limit.ts =====
type RateBucket = { count: number; resetAt: number };

const buckets = new Map<string, RateBucket>();

export function allowWrite(
  key: string,
  limit = 120,
  windowMs = 60_000,
): boolean {
  const now = Date.now();
  let bucket = buckets.get(key);
  if (!bucket || bucket.resetAt <= now) {
    if (buckets.size > 10_000) {
      for (const [currentKey, value] of buckets)
        if (value.resetAt <= now) buckets.delete(currentKey);
    }
    bucket = { count: 0, resetAt: now + windowMs };
    buckets.set(key, bucket);
  }
  bucket.count += 1;
  return bucket.count <= limit;
}


===== ./server/observability/logger.ts =====
export function logServerError(
  event: string,
  requestId: string,
  error: unknown,
): void {
  console.error(
    JSON.stringify({
      level: "error",
      event,
      requestId,
      errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : "UnknownError",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    }),
  );
}


===== ./server/repositories/drizzle-worksheet-repository.ts =====
import { and, desc, eq, isNull, or } from "drizzle-orm";
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
import { validateWorksheetDocument } from "../../domain/worksheet/validation";
import type { Database } from "../database/client";
import {
  auditEvents,
  worksheetPermissions,
  worksheetRevisions,
  worksheets,
} from "../database/schema";
import type {
  SaveOutcome,
  SaveWorksheetInput,
  WorksheetPermission,
  WorksheetRecord,
  WorksheetRepository,
  WorksheetRevisionSummary,
} from "./worksheet-repository";

export class DrizzleWorksheetRepository implements WorksheetRepository {
  constructor(private readonly database: Database) {}

  async create(
    ownerId: string,
    document: WorksheetRecord["document"],
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord> {
    const now = new Date();
    return this.database.transaction(async (transaction) => {
      const [inserted] = await transaction
        .insert(worksheets)
        .values({
          id: document.id,
          ownerId,
          title: document.title,
          schemaVersion: document.schemaVersion,
          revision: 1,
          content: document,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        })
        .onConflictDoNothing({ target: worksheets.id })
        .returning();

      if (!inserted) {
        const [existing] = await transaction
          .select()
          .from(worksheets)
          .where(eq(worksheets.id, document.id))
          .for("update")
          .limit(1);
        if (!existing || existing.ownerId !== ownerId || existing.deletedAt) {
          throw new Error("Worksheet ID is unavailable");
        }
        return toRecord(existing, ownerId, null);
      }

      await transaction.insert(worksheetRevisions).values({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        worksheetId: document.id,
        revisionNumber: 1,
        content: document,
        schemaVersion: document.schemaVersion,
        createdBy: ownerId,
        createdAt: now,
      });
      await transaction.insert(auditEvents).values({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        actorId: ownerId,
        resourceType: "worksheet",
        resourceId: document.id,
        action: "worksheet.created",
        metadata: { revision: 1 },
        createdAt: now,
      });
      return {
        document,
        ownerId,
        permission: "owner",
        revision: 1,
        updatedAt: now,
      };
    });
  }

  async listAccessible(userId: string): Promise<WorksheetRecord[]> {
    const rows = await this.database
      .select({
        worksheet: worksheets,
        permission: worksheetPermissions.permission,
      })
      .from(worksheets)
      .leftJoin(
        worksheetPermissions,
        and(
          eq(worksheetPermissions.worksheetId, worksheets.id),
          eq(worksheetPermissions.userId, userId),
        ),
      )
      .where(
        and(
          isNull(worksheets.deletedAt),
          or(
            eq(worksheets.ownerId, userId),
            eq(worksheetPermissions.userId, userId),
          ),
        ),
      )
      .orderBy(desc(worksheets.updatedAt));

    return rows.map(({ worksheet, permission }) =>
      toRecord(worksheet, userId, permission),
    );
  }

  async findAccessible(
    worksheetId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.database
      .select({
        worksheet: worksheets,
        permission: worksheetPermissions.permission,
      })
      .from(worksheets)
      .leftJoin(
        worksheetPermissions,
        and(
          eq(worksheetPermissions.worksheetId, worksheets.id),
          eq(worksheetPermissions.userId, userId),
        ),
      )
      .where(and(eq(worksheets.id, worksheetId), isNull(worksheets.deletedAt)))
      .limit(1);
    if (!row || (row.worksheet.ownerId !== userId && !row.permission))
      return null;
    return toRecord(row.worksheet, userId, row.permission);
  }

  async save(input: SaveWorksheetInput): Promise<SaveOutcome> {
    return this.database.transaction(
      async (transaction): Promise<SaveOutcome> => {
        const [current] = await transaction
          .select()
          .from(worksheets)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(worksheets.id, input.worksheetId),
              isNull(worksheets.deletedAt),
            ),
          )
          .for("update")
          .limit(1);
        if (!current) return { kind: "not-found" };

        const permission = await this.permissionFor(
          transaction,
          current.id,
          current.ownerId,
          input.actorId,
        );
        if (!permission || permission === "viewer")
          return { kind: "forbidden" };

        const [duplicateRequest] = await transaction
          .select()
          .from(worksheetRevisions)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(worksheetRevisions.worksheetId, current.id),
              eq(worksheetRevisions.requestId, input.requestId),
            ),
          )
          .limit(1);
        if (duplicateRequest) {
          if (!isDeepStrictEqual(duplicateRequest.content, input.document))
            return { kind: "idempotency-conflict" };
          return {
            kind: "saved",
            idempotent: true,
            record: {
              document: validateWorksheetDocument(duplicateRequest.content),
              ownerId: current.ownerId,
              permission,
              revision: duplicateRequest.revisionNumber,
              updatedAt: duplicateRequest.createdAt,
            },
          };
        }

        if (current.revision !== input.expectedRevision) {
          return {
            kind: "conflict",
            record: toRecord(
              current,
              input.actorId,
              permission === "owner" ? null : permission,
            ),
          };
        }

        const nextRevision = current.revision + 1;
        const now = new Date();
        const [updated] = await transaction
          .update(worksheets)
          .set({
            title: input.document.title,
            schemaVersion: input.document.schemaVersion,
            revision: nextRevision,
            content: input.document,
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(
            and(
              eq(worksheets.id, current.id),
              eq(worksheets.revision, input.expectedRevision),
            ),
          )
          .returning();

        if (!updated) {
          const [latest] = await transaction
            .select()
            .from(worksheets)
            .where(eq(worksheets.id, current.id))
            .limit(1);
          if (!latest) return { kind: "not-found" };
          return {
            kind: "conflict",
            record: toRecord(
              latest,
              input.actorId,
              permission === "owner" ? null : permission,
            ),
          };
        }

        await transaction.insert(worksheetRevisions).values({
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          worksheetId: current.id,
          revisionNumber: nextRevision,
          content: input.document,
          schemaVersion: input.document.schemaVersion,
          createdBy: input.actorId,
          requestId: input.requestId,
          createdAt: now,
        });
        await transaction.insert(auditEvents).values({
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          actorId: input.actorId,
          resourceType: "worksheet",
          resourceId: current.id,
          action: "worksheet.updated",
          metadata: { revision: nextRevision },
          createdAt: now,
        });

        return {
          kind: "saved",
          idempotent: false,
          record: {
            document: input.document,
            ownerId: updated.ownerId,
            permission,
            revision: nextRevision,
            updatedAt: now,
          },
        };
      },
    );
  }

  async softDelete(worksheetId: string, actorId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.database.transaction(async (transaction) => {
      const [deleted] = await transaction
        .update(worksheets)
        .set({ deletedAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() })
        .where(
          and(
            eq(worksheets.id, worksheetId),
            eq(worksheets.ownerId, actorId),
            isNull(worksheets.deletedAt),
          ),
        )
        .returning({ id: worksheets.id });
      if (!deleted) return false;
      await transaction.insert(auditEvents).values({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        actorId,
        resourceType: "worksheet",
        resourceId: worksheetId,
        action: "worksheet.deleted",
        metadata: {},
        createdAt: new Date(),
      });
      return true;
    });
  }

  async grantPermission(
    worksheetId: string,
    userId: string,
    permission: "editor" | "viewer",
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.database
      .insert(worksheetPermissions)
      .values({ worksheetId, userId, permission })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: [worksheetPermissions.worksheetId, worksheetPermissions.userId],
        set: { permission },
      });
  }

  async listRevisions(
    worksheetId: string,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRevisionSummary[]> {
    const rows = await this.database
      .select({
        revision: worksheetRevisions.revisionNumber,
        createdBy: worksheetRevisions.createdBy,
        createdAt: worksheetRevisions.createdAt,
      })
      .from(worksheetRevisions)
      .where(eq(worksheetRevisions.worksheetId, worksheetId))
      .orderBy(desc(worksheetRevisions.revisionNumber));
    return rows;
  }

  private async permissionFor(
    transaction: Parameters<Parameters<Database["transaction"]>[0]>[0],
    worksheetId: string,
    ownerId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<WorksheetPermission | null> {
    if (ownerId === userId) return "owner";
    const [row] = await transaction
      .select({ permission: worksheetPermissions.permission })
      .from(worksheetPermissions)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(worksheetPermissions.worksheetId, worksheetId),
          eq(worksheetPermissions.userId, userId),
        ),
      )
      .limit(1);
    return row?.permission ?? null;
  }
}

function toRecord(
  row: typeof worksheets.$inferSelect,
  userId: string,
  permission: "editor" | "viewer" | null,
): WorksheetRecord {
  const access: WorksheetPermission =
    row.ownerId === userId ? "owner" : (permission ?? "viewer");
  return {
    document: validateWorksheetDocument(row.content),
    ownerId: row.ownerId,
    permission: access,
    revision: row.revision,
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt,
  };
}


===== ./server/repositories/worksheet-repository.integration.test.ts =====
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import postgres from "postgres";
import { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../domain/expressions/parser";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../domain/worksheet/model";
import { schema, users, worksheets } from "../database/schema";
import { DrizzleWorksheetRepository } from "./drizzle-worksheet-repository";
import { WorksheetService } from "../services/worksheet-service";

const databaseUrl = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;
const integration = describe.skipIf(!databaseUrl);

integration("PostgreSQL worksheet repository", () => {
  let client: ReturnType<typeof postgres>;
  let database: PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>;
  let repository: DrizzleWorksheetRepository;
  let service: WorksheetService;
  let ownerId: string;
  let editorId: string;
  let viewerId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    client = postgres(databaseUrl!, { max: 1 });
    database = drizzle(client, { schema });
    repository = new DrizzleWorksheetRepository(database);
    service = new WorksheetService(repository);
    ownerId = crypto.randomUUID();
    editorId = crypto.randomUUID();
    viewerId = crypto.randomUUID();
    await database.insert(users).values(
      [ownerId, editorId, viewerId].map((id) => ({
        id,
        name: "Integration User",
        email: `${id}@example.invalid`,
        emailVerified: true,
      })),
    );
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (database && ownerId)
      await database.delete(worksheets).where(eq(worksheets.ownerId, ownerId));
    if (database && ownerId)
      await database.delete(users).where(eq(users.id, ownerId));
    if (database && editorId)
      await database.delete(users).where(eq(users.id, editorId));
    if (database && viewerId)
      await database.delete(users).where(eq(users.id, viewerId));
    if (client) await client.end({ timeout: 5 });
  });

  it("creates, retrieves, shares, saves revisions, and rejects stale updates", async () => {
    const initial = createDocument("initial");
    const created = await service.create(ownerId, initial);
    expect(created.revision).toBe(1);
    expect(await service.create(ownerId, initial)).toMatchObject({
      revision: 1,
      ownerId,
      document: { id: initial.id },
    });
    expect((await service.get(initial.id, ownerId)).permission).toBe("owner");
    await expect(
      service.get(initial.id, crypto.randomUUID()),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });

    await service.grantPermission(initial.id, ownerId, editorId, "editor");
    await service.grantPermission(initial.id, ownerId, viewerId, "viewer");
    expect((await service.get(initial.id, editorId)).permission).toBe("editor");
    expect((await service.get(initial.id, viewerId)).permission).toBe("viewer");

    const changed = { ...initial, title: "Updated calculation" };
    const saved = await service.save(
      initial.id,
      editorId,
      1,
      "save-editor-1",
      changed,
    );
    expect(saved.revision).toBe(2);
    expect(saved.document.title).toBe("Updated calculation");

    const retry = await service.save(
      initial.id,
      editorId,
      1,
      "save-editor-1",
      changed,
    );
    expect(retry.revision).toBe(2);
    await expect(
      service.save(initial.id, viewerId, 2, "save-viewer-1", changed),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403 });
    await expect(
      service.save(initial.id, ownerId, 1, "save-owner-stale", changed),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409,
      code: "revision_conflict",
      details: { currentRevision: 2 },
    });

    const revisions = await service.revisions(initial.id, viewerId);
    expect(revisions.map((revision) => revision.revision)).toEqual([2, 1]);
  });

  it("rolls back a worksheet insert when the owner foreign key is invalid", async () => {
    const document = createDocument("rollback");
    await expect(
      repository.create(crypto.randomUUID(), document),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(service.get(document.id, ownerId)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 404,
    });
  });
});

function createDocument(title: string): WorksheetDocument {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    title,
    settings: {
      page: { width: 210, height: 297, unit: "mm" },
      grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 },
    },
    regions: [
      {
        id: "torque",
        type: "math",
        content: parseMathStatement("torque := force * arm"),
        position: { x: 14.7, y: 62.37 },
        zIndex: 0,
      },
    ],
  };
}


===== ./server/repositories/worksheet-repository.ts =====
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../domain/worksheet/model";

export type WorksheetPermission = "owner" | "editor" | "viewer";

export type WorksheetRecord = {
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  ownerId: string;
  permission: WorksheetPermission;
  revision: number;
  updatedAt: Date;
};

export type SaveOutcome =
  | { kind: "saved"; record: WorksheetRecord; idempotent: boolean }
  | { kind: "conflict"; record: WorksheetRecord }
  | { kind: "forbidden" }
  | { kind: "not-found" }
  | { kind: "idempotency-conflict" };

export type SaveWorksheetInput = {
  worksheetId: string;
  actorId: string;
  expectedRevision: number;
  requestId: string;
  document: WorksheetDocument;
};

export type WorksheetRevisionSummary = {
  revision: number;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: Date;
};

export interface WorksheetRepository {
  create(
    ownerId: string,
    document: WorksheetDocument,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord>;
  listAccessible(userId: string): Promise<WorksheetRecord[]>;
  findAccessible(
    worksheetId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<WorksheetRecord | null>;
  save(input: SaveWorksheetInput): Promise<SaveOutcome>;
  softDelete(worksheetId: string, actorId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  grantPermission(
    worksheetId: string,
    userId: string,
    permission: "editor" | "viewer",
  ): Promise<void>;
  listRevisions(worksheetId: string): Promise<WorksheetRevisionSummary[]>;
}


===== ./README.md =====
# Forma

Forma is a browser-first, document-centered engineering worksheet foundation. It keeps worksheets as spatial documents with independently positioned math and text regions; it is not a vertically stacked code notebook. The current implementation is an architectural foundation, not Mathcad/SMath feature parity.

## Run locally

Requirements: Docker Engine with Compose. Start the app and PostgreSQL:

```sh
docker compose up --build
```

Open http://localhost:3000 and create an account. Compose applies checked-in Drizzle migrations before starting Next.js. The Compose database password and auth secret are development-only defaults.

For host-based commands, install dependencies with `npm ci` and configure `DATABASE_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET`, and `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` from `.env.example`. Never reuse the example secret outside local development.

## Implemented

- Versioned JSON worksheet document with Zod validation, millimeter page coordinates, stable region IDs, math/text discriminated regions, and legacy localStorage migration.
- MathLive direct mathematical editing behind Forma's adapter; the supported numeric subset converts to a structured AST. LaTeX is not the authoritative document format.
- Restricted numerical evaluator with dependency graph, order-independent assignments, duplicate-definition errors, circular dependency detection, structured errors, and dependent-only incremental recalculation.
- SI unit/quantity API with a tested initial subset and a source-versioned physical-constant registry. These are not yet parsed from worksheet math regions or connected to displayed calculation results.
- PostgreSQL JSONB persistence, owners, editor/viewer sharing, immutable revisions, optimistic concurrency, idempotent save retries, and local recovery keyed by user and base revision.
- Better Auth email/password accounts, server-side sessions and access checks, API request limits, security headers, health endpoints, Docker, unit/integration/E2E test foundations, and CI.

## Editing

Click the squared A4 page to add a math region. Drag its grip to move it. Use the region's variable field for assignments; edit the expression in place with MathLive. Text and math remain separate regions. Toolbox controls insert arithmetic, functions, and Greek symbols. `Ctrl+Space` in text switches to math; quote in an empty math region switches to text. Undo/redo, export, and local draft recovery are available.

Supported calculations are intentionally limited to real scalar arithmetic, assignments, powers, roots, and basic functions. Fractions, roots, powers, subscripts, and Greek-symbol shortcuts are editable; vector/matrix numerical evaluation, units in expressions, plots, rich text, and symbolic math are not complete.

## Checks

```sh
npm run typecheck
npm test
npm run test:integration
npm run test:e2e
npm run build
```

Integration tests require a separately migrated test database via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`. See [testing](docs/development/testing.md) and [local setup](docs/development/local-setup.md).

Architecture, module boundaries, security, database, deployment, and known limitations are documented under [docs](docs/architecture/overview.md).

COPY --from=builder --chown=forma:forma /app/.next/standalone ./

USER forma
EXPOSE 3000
CMD ["node", "server.js"]

import { parseMathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/parser";
import {
  DEFAULT_PAGE,
  type WorksheetDocument,
} from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";

export const starterDocument: WorksheetDocument = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "forma-starter",
  title: "Shaft fatigue worksheet",
  settings: { page: DEFAULT_PAGE, grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 } },
  regions: [
    {
      id: "heading",
      type: "text",
      content: "Shaft fatigue calculation",
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 20.79 },
      zIndex: 0,
      presentation: { role: "heading" },
    },
    {
      id: "description",
      type: "text",
      content: "Bending moment at the shoulder of a machined shaft",
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 38.61 },
      zIndex: 1,
    },
    {
      id: "load",
      type: "math",
      content: parseMathStatement("load := 142.35"),
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 62.37 },
      zIndex: 2,
    },
    {
      id: "arm",
      type: "math",

      position: { x: 14.7, y: 77.22 },
      zIndex: 3,
    },
    {
      id: "moment",
      type: "math",

      position: { x: 14.7, y: 92.07 },
      zIndex: 4,
    },
    {
      id: "note",

      content: "The result updates as the worksheet changes.",
