      zIndex: 5,
    },
  ],
};


===== ./features/worksheets/hooks/use-worksheet-session.ts =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { serializeWorksheet } from "../../../domain/worksheet/serialization";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";
import type { WorksheetApiRecord } from "../../../shared/contracts/worksheet-api";
import { DocumentHistory } from "../../editor/history/document-history";
import { EditorStateStore } from "../../editor/state/editor-state";
import { CalculationStateStore } from "../state/calculation-state";
import { starterDocument } from "../model/starter-document";
import { loadLocalRecovery, saveLocalRecovery } from "../state/recovery";

type PendingSave = { requestId: string; revision: number; document: string };

type Options = {
  userId: string;
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  history: DocumentHistory;
  calculationStore: CalculationStateStore;
  editorStore: EditorStateStore;
};

export function useWorksheetSession({
  userId,
  document,
  history,
  calculationStore,
  editorStore,
}: Options) {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState("Loading local recovery");
  const [cloudState, setCloudState] = useState("Connecting to Forma");
  const [baseRevision, setBaseRevision] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [worksheets, setWorksheets] = useState<WorksheetApiRecord[]>([]);
  const [syncConflict, setSyncConflict] = useState<WorksheetApiRecord | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [retryCount, setRetryCount] = useState(0);
  const [lastSaved, setLastSaved] = useState("");
  const initialWorksheetId = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());
  const lastSyncedDocument = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const pendingSave = useRef<PendingSave | null>(null);
  const saving = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    async function initialize() {
      let draft: ReturnType<typeof loadLocalRecovery> = null;
      try {
        draft = loadLocalRecovery(window.localStorage, userId);
        if (draft)
          installDocument(
            draft.document,
            history,
            calculationStore,
            editorStore,
          );

        const response = await fetch("/api/worksheets", { cache: "no-store" });
        const payload = await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord[] }>(
          response,
        );
        const records = payload.data;
        setWorksheets(records);
        let selected: WorksheetApiRecord;

        if (draft) {
          const matching = records.find(
            (record) => record.document.id === draft?.document.id,
          );
          if (matching) {
            selected = matching;
            lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(matching.document);
            if (draft.baseRevision !== matching.revision) {
              setSyncConflict(matching);
              setCloudState("Draft conflicts with a newer server revision");
            } else {
              setCloudState("Syncing recovered draft");
            }
          } else {
            selected = await createServerWorksheet({
              ...draft.document,
              id: initialWorksheetId.current,
              title: `${draft.document.title} (recovered)`.slice(0, 160),
            });
            installDocument(
              selected.document,
              history,
              calculationStore,
              editorStore,
            );
            lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(selected.document);
            setCloudState("Recovered as a new worksheet");
          }
        } else if (records[0]) {
          selected = records[0];
          installDocument(
            selected.document,
            history,
            calculationStore,
            editorStore,
          );
          lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(selected.document);
          setCloudState("Synced with server");
        } else {
          selected = await createServerWorksheet({
            ...starterDocument,
            id: initialWorksheetId.current,
          });
          installDocument(
            selected.document,
            history,
            calculationStore,
            editorStore,
          );
          lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(selected.document);
          setCloudState("Saved to server");
        }

        if (cancelled) return;
        setWorksheets((current) => [
          selected,
          ...current.filter(
            (record) => record.document.id !== selected.document.id,
          ),
        ]);
        setBaseRevision(selected.revision);
        setSaveState(draft ? "Recovered local draft" : "Ready");
      } catch (error) {
        setCloudState(
          error instanceof Error
            ? `Offline: ${error.message}`
            : "Offline; local recovery enabled",
        );
        setSaveState(
          draft ? "Recovered local draft" : "Local recovery enabled",
        );
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setReady(true);
      }
    }
    void initialize();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [calculationStore, editorStore, history, userId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        saveLocalRecovery(window.localStorage, document, baseRevision, userId);
        setLastSaved(
          new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {
            hour: "numeric",
            minute: "2-digit",
          }),
        );
        setSaveState("Saved locally");
      } catch {
        setSaveState("Unable to save recovery");
      }
    }, 350);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [baseRevision, document, ready, userId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready || baseRevision === null || syncConflict || saving.current)
      return;
    let serialized: string;
    try {
      serialized = serializeWorksheet(document);
    } catch {
      setCloudState("Worksheet needs valid content before saving");
      return;
    }
    if (serialized === lastSyncedDocument.current) return;

    const timeout = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      saving.current = true;
      const request =
        pendingSave.current?.revision === baseRevision &&
        pendingSave.current.document === serialized
          ? pendingSave.current
          : {
              requestId: crypto.randomUUID(),
              revision: baseRevision,
              document: serialized,
            };
      pendingSave.current = request;
      setCloudState("Saving to server");
      try {
        const response = await saveDocument(document, request);
        if (response.status === 409) {
          const latest = await fetch(
            `/api/worksheets/${encodeURIComponent(document.id)}`,
            { cache: "no-store" },
          );
          const current = await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord }>(
            latest,
          );
          setSyncConflict(current.data);
          setCloudState("Draft conflict; server was not overwritten");
          return;
        }
        const saved = await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord }>(
          response,
        );
        setBaseRevision(saved.data.revision);
        lastSyncedDocument.current = serialized;
        pendingSave.current = null;
        setWorksheets((current) => [
          saved.data,
          ...current.filter(
            (record) => record.document.id !== saved.data.document.id,
          ),
        ]);
        setCloudState("Saved to server");
      } catch (error) {
        setCloudState(
          error instanceof Error
            ? `Retrying: ${error.message}`
            : "Retrying server save",
        );
        window.setTimeout(() => setRetryCount((count) => count + 1), 3000);
      } finally {
        saving.current = false;
      }
    }, 650);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [baseRevision, document, ready, retryCount, syncConflict]);

  async function flushCurrentDocument(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (syncConflict || baseRevision === null || saving.current) return false;
    const serialized = serializeWorksheet(document);
    if (serialized === lastSyncedDocument.current) return true;

    saving.current = true;
    const request = {
      requestId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      revision: baseRevision,
      document: serialized,
    };
    pendingSave.current = request;
    setCloudState("Saving to server");
    try {
      const response = await saveDocument(document, request);
      if (response.status === 409) {
        const latest = await fetch(
          `/api/worksheets/${encodeURIComponent(document.id)}`,
          { cache: "no-store" },
        );
        const current = await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord }>(
          latest,
        );
        setSyncConflict(current.data);
        setCloudState("Draft conflict; server was not overwritten");
        return false;
      }
      const saved = await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord }>(response);
      setBaseRevision(saved.data.revision);
      lastSyncedDocument.current = serialized;
      pendingSave.current = null;
      setWorksheets((current) => [
        saved.data,
        ...current.filter(
          (record) => record.document.id !== saved.data.document.id,
        ),
      ]);
      setCloudState("Saved to server");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setCloudState(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unable to save worksheet",
      );
      return false;
    } finally {
      saving.current = false;
    }
  }

  async function openWorksheet(worksheetId: string) {
    const selected = worksheets.find(
      (record) => record.document.id === worksheetId,
    );
    if (
      !selected ||
      selected.document.id === document.id ||
      !(await flushCurrentDocument())
    )
      return;
    installDocument(selected.document, history, calculationStore, editorStore);
    lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(selected.document);
    pendingSave.current = null;
    setBaseRevision(selected.revision);
    setSyncConflict(null);
    setCloudState("Opened worksheet");
  }

  async function createWorksheet() {
    if (!(await flushCurrentDocument())) return;
    const created = await createServerWorksheet({
      ...starterDocument,
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      title: "Untitled worksheet",
    });
    setWorksheets((current) => [
      created,
      ...current.filter((record) => record.document.id !== created.document.id),
    ]);
    installDocument(created.document, history, calculationStore, editorStore);
    lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(created.document);
    pendingSave.current = null;
    setBaseRevision(created.revision);
    setSyncConflict(null);
    setCloudState("Saved to server");
  }

  async function reloadServerVersion() {
    if (!syncConflict) return;
    installDocument(
      syncConflict.document,
      history,
      calculationStore,
      editorStore,
    );
    lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(syncConflict.document);
    setBaseRevision(syncConflict.revision);
    pendingSave.current = null;
    setSyncConflict(null);
    setCloudState("Loaded the server version");
  }

  async function saveRecoveredCopy() {
    const recovered = {
      ...document,
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      title: `${document.title} (recovered copy)`.slice(0, 160),
    };
    try {
      const created = await createServerWorksheet(recovered);
      setWorksheets((current) => [
        created,
        ...current.filter(
          (record) => record.document.id !== created.document.id,
        ),
      ]);
      installDocument(created.document, history, calculationStore, editorStore);
      lastSyncedDocument.current = serializeWorksheet(created.document);
      setBaseRevision(created.revision);
      pendingSave.current = null;
      setSyncConflict(null);
      setCloudState("Recovered as a new worksheet");
    } catch (error) {
      setCloudState(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unable to save recovered copy",
      );
    }
  }

  return {
    ready,
    saveState,
    cloudState,
    baseRevision,
    worksheets,
    syncConflict,
    lastSaved,
    openWorksheet,
    createWorksheet,
    reloadServerVersion,
    saveRecoveredCopy,
  };
}

function installDocument(
  next: WorksheetDocument,
  history: DocumentHistory,
  calculationStore: CalculationStateStore,
  editorStore: EditorStateStore,
): void {
  history.replace(next);
  calculationStore.update(next);
  editorStore.update((state) => ({ ...state, activeRegionId: null }));
}

async function saveDocument(
  document: WorksheetDocument,
  request: PendingSave,
): Promise<Response> {
  return fetch(`/api/worksheets/${encodeURIComponent(document.id)}`, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Request-Id": request.requestId,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      document,
      baseRevision: request.revision,
      requestId: request.requestId,
    }),
  });
}

async function createServerWorksheet(
  document: WorksheetDocument,
): Promise<WorksheetApiRecord> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/worksheets", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Request-Id": crypto.randomUUID(),
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ document }),
  });
  return (await readResponse<{ data: WorksheetApiRecord }>(response)).data;
}

async function readResponse<T>(response: Response): Promise<T> {
  const payload: unknown = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) {
    const failure = payload as { error?: { message?: string } };
    throw new Error(failure.error?.message ?? "The server request failed");
  }
  return payload as T;
}


===== ./features/worksheets/components/worksheet-toolbox.tsx =====
"use client";

import { FunctionSquare, Plus, Type } from "lucide-react";

const keypad = [
  "7",
  "8",
  "9",
  "+",
  "4",
  "5",
  "6",
  "-",
  "1",
  "2",
  "3",
  "*",
  "0",
  ".",
  "(",
  ")",
  "/",
  "^",
  "pi",
  "sqrt",
];
const functions = ["sin", "cos", "tan", "ln", "log", "exp", "abs"];
const symbols = ["alpha", "beta", "theta", "sigma", "phi", "omega"];

type Props = {
  onAddRegion(type: "math" | "text"): void;
  onToken(token: string): void;
  onFunction(name: string): void;
};

export function WorksheetToolbox({ onAddRegion, onToken, onFunction }: Props) {
  return (
    <aside className="toolbox" aria-label="Math toolbox">
      <div className="toolbox-heading">WORKSHEET TOOLS</div>
      <div className="region-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="region-tool"
          onClick={() => onAddRegion("math")}
        >
          <FunctionSquare size={15} /> Math region
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="region-tool"
          onClick={() => onAddRegion("text")}
        >
          <Type size={15} /> Text region
        </button>
      </div>
      <section className="tool-group">
        <h2>Arithmetic</h2>
        <div className="keypad">
          {keypad.map((token) => (
            <button key={token} type="button" onClick={() => onToken(token)}>
              {token}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="tool-group">
        <h2>Functions</h2>
        <div className="function-keys">
          {functions.map((name) => (
            <button key={name} type="button" onClick={() => onFunction(name)}>
              {name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="tool-group symbol-group">
        <h2>Greek symbols</h2>
        <div className="function-keys">
          {symbols.map((name) => (
            <button
              key={name}
              type="button"
              aria-label={`Insert ${name}`}
              onClick={() => onToken(`\\${name}`)}
            >
              {greekLetter(name)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <div className="toolbox-hint">
        <span>sigma</span> inserts σ <span>Ctrl + Space</span> switches text to
        math
      </div>
      <div className="toolbox-footer">
        <Plus size={13} /> Click anywhere on the paper to add a math region
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

function greekLetter(name: string): string {
  const letters: Record<string, string> = {
    alpha: "α",
    beta: "β",
    theta: "θ",
    sigma: "σ",
    phi: "φ",
    omega: "ω",
  };
  return letters[name] ?? name;
}


===== ./features/worksheets/components/worksheet-canvas.tsx =====
"use client";

import type { MouseEvent, PointerEvent, RefObject } from "react";
import type { CalculationResult } from "../../../domain/calculation/types";
import type {
  WorksheetDocument,
  WorksheetRegion,
  Point,
} from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";
import type { MathEditorHandle } from "../../editor/math/editor-port";
import { WorksheetRegionView } from "../../regions/worksheet-region";

type Props = {
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  calculation: CalculationResult;
  activeRegionId: string | null;
  canvasRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
  dragPosition?: Point;
  dragRegionId?: string;
  onPaperClick(event: MouseEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
  onActivate(regionId: string): void;
  onDelete(regionId: string): void;
  onReplace(region: WorksheetRegion): void;
  onDragStart(
    event: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>,
    region: WorksheetRegion,
  ): void;
  onMathEditorReady(regionId: string, handle: MathEditorHandle | null): void;
  onRequestText(region: WorksheetRegion): void;
  onRequestMath(region: WorksheetRegion): void;
};

export function WorksheetCanvas({
  document,
  calculation,
  activeRegionId,
  canvasRef,
  dragPosition,
  dragRegionId,
  onPaperClick,
  onActivate,
  onDelete,
  onReplace,
  onDragStart,
  onMathEditorReady,
  onRequestText,
  onRequestMath,
}: Props) {
  return (
    <section className="editor-scroll" aria-label="Worksheet canvas">
      <article
        className="worksheet-page"
        aria-label="Squared worksheet paper"
        ref={canvasRef}
        onClick={onPaperClick}
      >
        {document.regions.map((region, index) => {
          const result =
            region.type === "math" ? calculation.regions[region.id] : undefined;
          return (
            <WorksheetRegionView
              key={region.id}
              region={region}
              index={index}
              active={activeRegionId === region.id}
              result={result}
              error={result?.status === "error" ? result.message : undefined}
              onActivate={() => onActivate(region.id)}
              onDelete={() => onDelete(region.id)}
              onReplace={onReplace}
              onDragStart={(event) => onDragStart(event, region)}
              onMathEditorReady={(handle) =>
                onMathEditorReady(region.id, handle)
              }
              onRequestText={() => onRequestText(region)}
              onRequestMath={() => onRequestMath(region)}
              position={dragRegionId === region.id ? dragPosition : undefined}
            />
          );
        })}
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}

===== ./features/worksheets/components/worksheet-workspace.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type MouseEvent,
  type PointerEvent,
} from "react";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/parser";
import type { MathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/ast";
import type { Point, WorksheetRegion } from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";
import { DocumentHistory } from "../../editor/history/document-history";
import type { MathEditorHandle } from "../../editor/math/editor-port";
import { EditorStateStore } from "../../editor/state/editor-state";
import { authClient } from "../../auth/auth-client";
import { starterDocument } from "../model/starter-document";
import { CalculationStateStore } from "../state/calculation-state";
import { useWorksheetSession } from "../hooks/use-worksheet-session";
import { WorksheetCanvas } from "./worksheet-canvas";
import { WorksheetConflictBanner } from "./worksheet-conflict-banner";
import { WorksheetHeader } from "./worksheet-header";
import { WorksheetToolbox } from "./worksheet-toolbox";

const PAGE_WIDTH_MM = 210;
const PAGE_HEIGHT_MM = 297;

type DragState = { regionId: string; pointerId: number; position: Point };

export function WorksheetWorkspace({ userId }: { userId: string }) {
  const [history] = useState(() => new DocumentHistory(starterDocument));
  const [editorStore] = useState(() => new EditorStateStore());
  const [calculationStore] = useState(
    () => new CalculationStateStore(starterDocument),
  );
  const historyState = useSyncExternalStore(
    history.subscribe,
    history.getSnapshot,
    history.getSnapshot,
  );
  const editorState = useSyncExternalStore(
    editorStore.subscribe,
    editorStore.getSnapshot,
    editorStore.getSnapshot,
  );
  const calculationState = useSyncExternalStore(
    calculationStore.subscribe,
    calculationStore.getSnapshot,
    calculationStore.getSnapshot,
  );
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState | null>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const editorHandles = useRef(new Map<string, MathEditorHandle>());
  const pendingInsertion = useRef<{ regionId: string; latex: string } | null>(
    null,
  );
  const dragPosition = useRef<DragState | null>(null);
  const document = historyState.document;
  const calculation = calculationState.result;
  const session = useWorksheetSession({
    userId,
    document,
    history,
    calculationStore,
    editorStore,
  });
  const {
    ready,
    saveState,
    cloudState,
    worksheets,
    syncConflict,
    lastSaved,
    openWorksheet,
    createWorksheet,
    reloadServerVersion,
    saveRecoveredCopy,
  } = session;

  useEffect(
    () =>
      history.subscribe(() =>
        calculationStore.update(history.getSnapshot().document),
      ),
    [calculationStore, history],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleHistory(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (!(event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) || event.key.toLowerCase() !== "z")
        return;
      if (
        (event.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName.toLowerCase() ===
        "math-field"
      )
        return;
      event.preventDefault();
      if (event.shiftKey) history.redo();
      else history.undo();
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleHistory);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleHistory);
  }, [history]);

  function executeReplace(region: WorksheetRegion) {
    history.execute({ type: "replace-region", region }, `region:${region.id}`);
  }

  function addRegion(
    type: "math" | "text",
    position: Point,
    initialLatex = "",
  ) {
    const regionId = `region-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    const region: WorksheetRegion =
      type === "math"
        ? {
            id: regionId,
            type,
            content: { kind: "expression", expression: { kind: "empty" } },
            position,
            zIndex: document.regions.length,
          }
        : {
            id: regionId,
            type,
            content: "",
            position,
            zIndex: document.regions.length,
          };
    history.execute({ type: "insert-region", region });
    editorStore.update((state) => ({ ...state, activeRegionId: regionId }));
    if (initialLatex)
      pendingInsertion.current = { regionId, latex: initialLatex };
  }

  function addAtEnd(type: "math" | "text") {
    const lastY = document.regions.reduce(
      (max, region) => Math.max(max, region.position.y),
      14,
    );
    addRegion(type, {
      x: 14.7,
      y: lastY + 18 < PAGE_HEIGHT_MM - 10 ? lastY + 18 : 20,
    });
  }

  function handlePaperClick(event: MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) {
    if (event.target !== event.currentTarget || !canvasRef.current) return;
    const bounds = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    addRegion("math", {
      x: clamp(
        ((event.clientX - bounds.left) / bounds.width) * PAGE_WIDTH_MM,
        4,
        PAGE_WIDTH_MM - 8,
      ),
      y: clamp(
        ((event.clientY - bounds.top) / bounds.height) * PAGE_HEIGHT_MM,
        4,
        PAGE_HEIGHT_MM - 8,
      ),
    });
  }

  function handleMathEditorReady(
    regionId: string,
    handle: MathEditorHandle | null,
  ) {
    if (handle) {
      editorHandles.current.set(regionId, handle);
      if (pendingInsertion.current?.regionId === regionId) {
        handle.insert(pendingInsertion.current.latex);
        pendingInsertion.current = null;
      }
    } else {
      editorHandles.current.delete(regionId);
    }
  }

  function handleToken(token: string) {
    const latex =
      token === "pi"
        ? "\\pi"
        : token === "sqrt"
          ? "\\sqrt{\\placeholder{}}"
          : token;
    const activeId = editorState.activeRegionId;
    const active = document.regions.find((region) => region.id === activeId);
    if (active?.type === "math" && editorHandles.current.has(active.id)) {
      editorHandles.current.get(active.id)?.insert(latex);
      return;
    }
    const position = active
      ? {
          x: active.position.x,
          y: Math.min(active.position.y + 12, PAGE_HEIGHT_MM - 10),
        }
      : { x: 14.7, y: 20 };
    addRegion("math", position, latex);
  }

  function handleFunction(name: string) {
    const latex = `\\${name}\\left(\\placeholder{}\\right)`;
    const activeId = editorState.activeRegionId;
    const active = document.regions.find((region) => region.id === activeId);
    if (active?.type === "math" && editorHandles.current.has(active.id)) {
      editorHandles.current.get(active.id)?.insert(latex);
      return;
    }
    addRegion(
      "math",
      active
        ? { x: active.position.x, y: active.position.y + 12 }
        : { x: 14.7, y: 20 },
      latex,
    );
  }

  function requestText(region: WorksheetRegion) {
    if (region.type !== "math" || region.content.expression.kind !== "empty")
      return;
    history.execute({
      type: "replace-region",
      region: { ...region, type: "text", content: "" },
    });
  }

  function requestMath(region: WorksheetRegion) {
    if (region.type !== "text") return;
    let content: MathStatement;
    try {
      content = parseMathStatement(region.content);
    } catch {
      content = { kind: "expression", expression: { kind: "empty" } };
    }
    history.execute({
      type: "replace-region",
      region: { ...region, type: "math", content },
    });
  }

  function startDrag(
    event: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>,
    region: WorksheetRegion,
  ) {
    if (!canvasRef.current) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    const pointerId = event.pointerId;
    const bounds = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const move = (moveEvent: globalThis.PointerEvent) => {
      if (moveEvent.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
      const next = {
        regionId: region.id,
        pointerId,
        position: {
          x: clamp(
            ((moveEvent.clientX - bounds.left) / bounds.width) * PAGE_WIDTH_MM,
            0,
            PAGE_WIDTH_MM - 4,
          ),
          y: clamp(
            ((moveEvent.clientY - bounds.top) / bounds.height) * PAGE_HEIGHT_MM,
            0,
            PAGE_HEIGHT_MM - 4,
          ),
        },
      };
      dragPosition.current = next;
      setDrag(next);
    };
    const finish = (upEvent: globalThis.PointerEvent) => {
      if (upEvent.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", finish);
      const current = dragPosition.current;
      if (current?.regionId === region.id)
        history.execute({
          type: "move-region",
          regionId: region.id,
          position: current.position,
        });
      dragPosition.current = null;
      setDrag(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", finish);
  }

  function exportWorksheet() {
    const file = new Blob([JSON.stringify(document, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    const link = window.document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${
      document.title
        .trim()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, "-")
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, "") || "worksheet"
    }.json`;
    link.click();
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
  }

  function resetWorksheet() {
    if (!window.confirm("Reset this worksheet to the starter calculation?"))
      return;
    history.replace({ ...starterDocument, id: document.id });
    editorStore.update((state) => ({ ...state, activeRegionId: "moment" }));
  }

  return (
    <main className="app-shell">
      <WorksheetHeader
        document={document}
        worksheets={worksheets}
        cloudState={cloudState}
        saveState={saveState}
        lastSaved={lastSaved}
        canUndo={historyState.canUndo}
        canRedo={historyState.canRedo}
        disabled={!ready || Boolean(syncConflict)}
        onOpenWorksheet={(id) => void openWorksheet(id)}
        onTitleChange={(title) =>
          history.execute(
            { type: "rename-worksheet", title },
            "worksheet:title",
          )
        }
        onNewWorksheet={() => void createWorksheet()}
        onUndo={() => history.undo()}
        onRedo={() => history.redo()}
        onReset={resetWorksheet}
        onExport={exportWorksheet}
        onSignOut={async () => {
          await authClient.signOut();
          window.location.assign("/sign-in");
        }}
      />

      {syncConflict && (
        <WorksheetConflictBanner
          conflict={syncConflict}
          onReload={reloadServerVersion}
          onSaveCopy={() => void saveRecoveredCopy()}
        />
      )}

      <div className="workspace">
        <WorksheetCanvas
          document={document}
          calculation={calculation}
          activeRegionId={editorState.activeRegionId}
          canvasRef={canvasRef}
          dragRegionId={drag?.regionId}
          dragPosition={drag?.position}
          onPaperClick={handlePaperClick}
          onActivate={(regionId) =>
            editorStore.update((state) => ({
              ...state,
              activeRegionId: regionId,
            }))
          }
          onDelete={(regionId) =>
            history.execute({ type: "delete-region", regionId })
          }
          onReplace={executeReplace}
          onDragStart={(event, region) => startDrag(event, region)}
          onMathEditorReady={handleMathEditorReady}
          onRequestText={requestText}
          onRequestMath={requestMath}
        />
        <WorksheetToolbox
          onAddRegion={addAtEnd}
          onToken={handleToken}
          onFunction={handleFunction}
        />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}


===== ./features/worksheets/state/recovery.ts =====
import {
  deserializeWorksheet,
  serializeWorksheet,
} from "../../../domain/worksheet/serialization";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";

const currentStorageKey = "forma-worksheet-recovery-v1";
const previousStorageKey = "forma-worksheet-v2";
const legacyStorageKey = "forma-worksheet-v1";

function scopedKey(scope: string | undefined): string {
  return scope ? `${currentStorageKey}:${scope}` : currentStorageKey;
}

export type LocalWorksheetDraft = {
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  baseRevision: number | null;
};

export function loadLocalRecovery(
  storage: Storage,
  scope?: string,
): LocalWorksheetDraft | null {
  const current = storage.getItem(scopedKey(scope));
  if (current) {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(current);
    if (isRecoveryEnvelope(parsed)) {
      return {
        document: deserializeWorksheet(JSON.stringify(parsed.document)),
        baseRevision: parsed.baseRevision,
      };
    }
    return { document: deserializeWorksheet(current), baseRevision: null };
  }
  const previous = storage.getItem(previousStorageKey);
  if (previous)
    return { document: deserializeWorksheet(previous), baseRevision: null };
  const legacy = storage.getItem(legacyStorageKey);
  if (legacy)
    return { document: deserializeWorksheet(legacy), baseRevision: null };
  return null;
}

export function saveLocalRecovery(
  storage: Storage,
  document: WorksheetDocument,
  baseRevision: number | null,
  scope?: string,
): void {
  storage.setItem(
    scopedKey(scope),
    JSON.stringify({
      recoveryVersion: 1,
      baseRevision,
      document: JSON.parse(serializeWorksheet(document)) as WorksheetDocument,
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    }),
  );
}

export function clearLocalRecovery(storage: Storage, scope?: string): void {
  storage.removeItem(scopedKey(scope));
  storage.removeItem(legacyStorageKey);
  storage.removeItem(previousStorageKey);
}

function isRecoveryEnvelope(value: unknown): value is {
  recoveryVersion: 1;
  baseRevision: number | null;
  document: unknown;
} {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    "recoveryVersion" in value &&
    value.recoveryVersion === 1 &&
    "document" in value &&
    "baseRevision" in value &&
    (value.baseRevision === null ||
      (typeof value.baseRevision === "number" &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(value.baseRevision) &&
        value.baseRevision >= 1))
  );
}


===== ./features/worksheets/state/recovery.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { starterDocument } from "../model/starter-document";
import { loadLocalRecovery, saveLocalRecovery } from "./recovery";

class MemoryStorage implements Storage {
  private values = new Map<string, string>();
  get length() {
    return this.values.size;
  }
  clear() {
    this.values.clear();
  }
  getItem(key: string) {
    return this.values.get(key) ?? null;
  }
  key(index: number) {
    return [...this.values.keys()][index] ?? null;
  }
  removeItem(key: string) {
    this.values.delete(key);
  }
  setItem(key: string, value: string) {
    this.values.set(key, value);
  }
}

describe("local worksheet recovery", () => {
  it("round-trips an AST document with its optimistic base revision", () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    saveLocalRecovery(storage, starterDocument, 4);
    expect(loadLocalRecovery(storage)).toEqual({
      document: starterDocument,
      baseRevision: 4,
    });
  });

  it("loads old prototype storage as a draft without a trusted base revision", () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    storage.setItem(
      "forma-worksheet-v1",
      JSON.stringify({
        title: "Recovered calculation",
        regions: [
          { id: "load", type: "math", content: "load := 12", x: 7, y: 8 },
        ],
      }),
    );
    expect(loadLocalRecovery(storage)).toMatchObject({
      baseRevision: null,
      document: {
        title: "Recovered calculation",
        regions: [{ content: { kind: "assignment", name: "load" } }],
      },
    });
  });
});


===== ./features/worksheets/state/calculation-state.ts =====
import { CalculationSession } from "../../../domain/calculation/engine";
import type { CalculationResult } from "../../../domain/calculation/types";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";

export type CalculationStateSnapshot = {
  documentId: string;
  result: CalculationResult;
};

export class CalculationStateStore {
  private readonly session = new CalculationSession();
  private snapshot: CalculationStateSnapshot;
  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  constructor(initialDocument: WorksheetDocument) {
    this.snapshot = {
      documentId: initialDocument.id,
      result: this.session.calculate(initialDocument),
    };
  }

  getSnapshot = (): CalculationStateSnapshot => this.snapshot;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };

  update(document: WorksheetDocument): void {
    this.snapshot = {
      documentId: document.id,
      result: this.session.calculate(document),
    };
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
}


===== ./features/worksheets/state/calculation-state.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/parser";
import { starterDocument } from "../model/starter-document";
import { CalculationStateStore } from "./calculation-state";

describe("calculation state store", () => {
  it("updates derived results outside render and reuses unaffected regions", () => {
    const store = new CalculationStateStore(starterDocument);
    const listener = vi.fn();
    store.subscribe(listener);
    const changed = {
      ...starterDocument,
      regions: starterDocument.regions.map((region) =>
        region.id === "load" && region.type === "math"
          ? { ...region, content: parseMathStatement("load := 150") }
          : region,
      ),
    };

    store.update(changed);
    expect(store.getSnapshot().result.regions.moment).toEqual({
      status: "success",
      value: 418.5,
    });
    expect(new Set(store.getSnapshot().result.evaluatedRegionIds)).toEqual(
      new Set(["load", "moment"]),
    );
    expect(listener).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});


===== ./features/regions/worksheet-region.tsx =====
"use client";

import { memo } from "react";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import type { RegionCalculationResult } from "../../domain/calculation/types";
import type { MathStatement } from "../../domain/expressions/ast";
import type { Point, WorksheetRegion } from "../../domain/worksheet/model";
import type { MathEditorHandle } from "../editor/math/editor-port";
import { MathLiveEditor } from "../editor/math/mathlive-editor";
import { formatCalculationResult } from "./format-result";
import type {
  PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent,
  KeyboardEvent as ReactKeyboardEvent,
} from "react";

export type WorksheetRegionProps = {
  region: WorksheetRegion;
  index: number;
  active: boolean;
  result?: RegionCalculationResult;
  error?: string;
  onActivate(): void;
  onDelete(): void;
  onReplace(region: WorksheetRegion): void;
  onDragStart(event: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>): void;
  onMathEditorReady(handle: MathEditorHandle | null): void;
  onRequestText(): void;
  onRequestMath(): void;
  position?: Point;
};

export const WorksheetRegionView = memo(function WorksheetRegionView({
  region,
  index,
  active,
  result,
  error,
  onActivate,
  onDelete,
  onReplace,
  onDragStart,
  onMathEditorReady,
  onRequestText,
  onRequestMath,
  position,
}: WorksheetRegionProps) {
  const className = `worksheet-region ${region.type}-region ${active ? "active" : ""} ${error ? "has-error" : ""} ${region.type === "text" && region.presentation?.role === "heading" ? "heading-region" : ""}`;

  function updateMath(expression: MathStatement["expression"]) {
    if (region.type !== "math") return;
    const content: MathStatement =
      region.content.kind === "assignment"
        ? { ...region.content, expression }
        : { kind: "expression", expression };
    onReplace({ ...region, content });
  }

  function updateDefinition(name: string) {
    if (region.type !== "math") return;
    const expression = region.content.expression;
    const content: MathStatement = name.trim()
      ? { kind: "assignment", name: name.trim(), expression }
      : { kind: "expression", expression };
    onReplace({ ...region, content });
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={className}
      style={{
        left: `${((position ?? region.position).x / 210) * 100}%`,
        top: `${((position ?? region.position).y / 297) * 100}%`,
        zIndex: region.zIndex,
      }}
      onClick={(event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        onActivate();
      }}
    >
      <button
        className="region-grip"
        type="button"
        title="Move region"
        aria-label={`Move region ${index + 1}`}
        onPointerDown={onDragStart}
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true">{index + 1}</span>
      </button>
      {region.type === "math" ? (
        <>
          <label className="definition-input">
            <span className="visually-hidden">Variable name</span>
            <input
              aria-label={`Variable definition ${index + 1}`}
              value={
                region.content.kind === "assignment" ? region.content.name : ""
              }
              onChange={(event) => updateDefinition(event.target.value)}
              placeholder=""
            />
            <span aria-hidden="true">
              {region.content.kind === "assignment" ? ":=" : ""}
            </span>
          </label>
          <MathLiveEditor
            expression={region.content.expression}
            onChange={updateMath}
            onRequestText={onRequestText}
            onReady={onMathEditorReady}
          />
          {result?.status === "success" && (
            <>
              <span className="equals-sign">=</span>
              <output className="inline-result">
                {formatCalculationResult(result.value)}
              </output>
            </>
          )}
          {result?.status === "error" && (
            <span className="inline-error" role="status">
              {result.message}
            </span>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <textarea
          aria-label={`Text region ${index + 1}`}
          className="text-input"
          value={region.content}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onReplace({ ...region, content: event.target.value })
          }
          onKeyDown={(event: ReactKeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
            if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.code === "Space") {
              event.preventDefault();
              onRequestMath();
            } else if (event.code === "Space" && !region.content) {
              event.preventDefault();
              onRequestMath();
            }
          }}
          placeholder="Type text..."
          rows={Math.max(1, Math.min(5, region.content.split("\n").length))}
        />
      )}
      <button
        className="delete-region"
        type="button"
        title="Delete region"
        aria-label={`Delete region ${index + 1}`}
        onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          onDelete();
        }}
      >
        <Trash2 size={13} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
});


===== ./features/regions/format-result.ts =====
export function formatCalculationResult(value: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    maximumSignificantDigits: 10,
  }).format(value);
}


===== ./features/auth/sign-in-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { authClient } from "./auth-client";

export function SignInForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"sign-in" | "sign-up">("sign-in");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage("");
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const email = String(form.get("email") ?? "");
    const password = String(form.get("password") ?? "");
    const name = String(form.get("name") ?? "");
    const result =
      mode === "sign-up"
        ? await authClient.signUp.email({ email, password, name })
        : await authClient.signIn.email({ email, password });
    setBusy(false);
    if (result.error) {
      setMessage(result.error.message ?? "Unable to authenticate");
      return;
    }
    router.replace("/");
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <main className="auth-shell">
      <section className="auth-panel">
        <Link className="auth-brand" href="/" aria-label="Forma home">
          <span className="brand-mark">f</span>
          <span className="brand-name">forma</span>
        </Link>
        <p className="auth-kicker">ENGINEERING WORKSHEETS</p>
        <h1>
          {mode === "sign-in" ? "Sign in to Forma" : "Create your account"}
        </h1>
        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          {mode === "sign-up" && (
            <label>
              Name
              <input name="name" autoComplete="name" required maxLength={120} />
            </label>
          )}
          <label>
            Email
            <input
              name="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              required
              maxLength={254}
            />
          </label>
          <label>
            Password
            <input
              name="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete={
                mode === "sign-up" ? "new-password" : "current-password"
              }
              required
              minLength={8}
              maxLength={128}
            />
          </label>
          {message && (
            <p className="auth-error" role="alert">
              {message}
            </p>
          )}
          <button type="submit" disabled={busy}>
            {busy
              ? "Working..."
              : mode === "sign-in"
                ? "Sign in"
                : "Create account"}
          </button>
        </form>
        <button
          className="auth-switch"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setMessage("");
            setMode(mode === "sign-in" ? "sign-up" : "sign-in");
          }}
        >
          {mode === "sign-in"
            ? "New to Forma? Create an account"
            : "Already have an account? Sign in"}
        </button>
      </section>
      <div className="auth-paper" aria-hidden="true">
        <span>σ =</span>
        <span className="auth-fraction">
          T r<small>J</small>
        </span>
        <i />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

===== ./features/editor/commands/worksheet-command.ts =====
import type {
  WorksheetDocument,
  WorksheetRegion,
} from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";

export type WorksheetCommand =
  | { type: "insert-region"; region: WorksheetRegion }
  | { type: "replace-region"; region: WorksheetRegion }
  | { type: "delete-region"; regionId: string }
  | {
      type: "move-region";
      regionId: string;
      position: { x: number; y: number };
    }
  | { type: "rename-worksheet"; title: string };

export function applyWorksheetCommand(
  document: WorksheetDocument,
  command: WorksheetCommand,
): WorksheetDocument {
  switch (command.type) {
    case "insert-region":
      if (document.regions.some((region) => region.id === command.region.id))
        throw new Error("Region ID already exists");
      return { ...document, regions: [...document.regions, command.region] };
    case "replace-region":
      return {
        ...document,
        regions: document.regions.map((region) =>
          region.id === command.region.id ? command.region : region,
        ),
      };
    case "delete-region":
      return {
        ...document,
        regions: document.regions.filter(
          (region) => region.id !== command.regionId,
        ),
      };
    case "move-region":
      return {
        ...document,
        regions: document.regions.map((region) =>
          region.id === command.regionId
            ? { ...region, position: command.position }
            : region,
        ),
      };
    case "rename-worksheet":
      return { ...document, title: command.title };
  }
}


===== ./features/editor/history/document-history.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  DEFAULT_PAGE,
  type WorksheetDocument,
} from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";
import { DocumentHistory } from "./document-history";

const document: WorksheetDocument = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "history-test",
  title: "Initial",
  settings: { page: DEFAULT_PAGE, grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 } },
  regions: [],
};

describe("document command history", () => {
  it("supports command execution, undo, redo, and future invalidation", () => {
    const history = new DocumentHistory(document);
    history.execute({ type: "rename-worksheet", title: "Edited" });
    expect(history.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({
      document: { title: "Edited" },
      canUndo: true,
      canRedo: false,
    });
    history.undo();
    expect(history.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({
      document: { title: "Initial" },
      canUndo: false,
      canRedo: true,
    });
    history.redo();
    history.undo();
    history.execute({ type: "rename-worksheet", title: "Another edit" });
    expect(history.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({
      document: { title: "Another edit" },
      canRedo: false,
    });
  });

  it("bounds retained history", () => {
    const history = new DocumentHistory(document, 2);
    history.execute({ type: "rename-worksheet", title: "1" });
    history.execute({ type: "rename-worksheet", title: "2" });
    history.execute({ type: "rename-worksheet", title: "3" });
    history.undo();
    history.undo();
    expect(history.getSnapshot()).toMatchObject({
      document: { title: "1" },
      canUndo: false,
    });
  });

  it("groups rapid edits to one target into a single undo step", () => {
    const history = new DocumentHistory(document);
    history.execute(
      { type: "rename-worksheet", title: "S" },
      "worksheet:title",
    );
    history.execute(
      { type: "rename-worksheet", title: "Sh" },
      "worksheet:title",
    );
    history.execute(
      { type: "rename-worksheet", title: "Shaft" },
      "worksheet:title",
    );
    history.undo();
    expect(history.getSnapshot().document.title).toBe("Initial");
  });
});


===== ./features/editor/history/document-history.ts =====
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../../domain/worksheet/model";
import {
  applyWorksheetCommand,
  type WorksheetCommand,
} from "../commands/worksheet-command";

export type HistorySnapshot = {
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
};

export class DocumentHistory {
  private document: WorksheetDocument;
  private past: WorksheetDocument[] = [];
  private future: WorksheetDocument[] = [];
  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  private snapshot: HistorySnapshot;
  private lastCoalesce: { key: string; at: number } | null = null;

  constructor(
    initialDocument: WorksheetDocument,
    private readonly limit = 100,
  ) {
    this.document = initialDocument;
    this.snapshot = {
      document: initialDocument,
      canUndo: false,
      canRedo: false,
    };
  }

  getSnapshot = (): HistorySnapshot => this.snapshot;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };

  execute(command: WorksheetCommand, coalesceKey?: string): void {
    const next = applyWorksheetCommand(this.document, command);
    if (next === this.document) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    const coalesce =
      coalesceKey &&
      this.lastCoalesce?.key === coalesceKey &&
      now - this.lastCoalesce.at < 750;
    if (!coalesce) this.past = [...this.past, this.document].slice(-this.limit);
    this.document = next;
    this.future = [];
    this.lastCoalesce = coalesceKey ? { key: coalesceKey, at: now } : null;
    this.emit();
  }

  replace(document: WorksheetDocument): void {
    this.document = document;
    this.past = [];
    this.future = [];
    this.lastCoalesce = null;
    this.emit();
  }

  undo(): void {
    const previous = this.past.at(-1);
    if (!previous) return;
    this.past = this.past.slice(0, -1);
    this.future = [this.document, ...this.future].slice(0, this.limit);
    this.document = previous;
    this.lastCoalesce = null;
    this.emit();
  }

  redo(): void {
    const next = this.future[0];
    if (!next) return;
    this.future = this.future.slice(1);
    this.past = [...this.past, this.document].slice(-this.limit);
    this.document = next;
    this.lastCoalesce = null;
    this.emit();
  }

  private emit(): void {
    this.snapshot = {
      document: this.document,
      canUndo: this.past.length > 0,
      canRedo: this.future.length > 0,
    };
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
}


===== ./features/editor/math/mathlive-adapter.ts =====
import type { MathExpression } from "../../../domain/expressions/ast";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/parser";
import type {
  MathEditorAdapter,
  MathEditorCallbacks,
  MathEditorHandle,
} from "./editor-port";

const greekShortcuts = {
  alpha: "\\alpha",
  beta: "\\beta",
  delta: "\\delta",
  gamma: "\\gamma",
  lambda: "\\lambda",
  mu: "\\mu",
  omega: "\\omega",
  rho: "\\rho",
  sigma: "\\sigma",
  theta: "\\theta",
  tau: "\\tau",
  phi: "\\phi",
};

const greekNames: Record<string, string> = {
  alpha: "\\alpha",
  beta: "\\beta",
  delta: "\\delta",
  gamma: "\\gamma",
  lambda: "\\lambda",
  mu: "\\mu",
  omega: "\\omega",
  rho: "\\rho",
  sigma: "\\sigma",
  theta: "\\theta",
  tau: "\\tau",
  phi: "\\phi",
};

export class MathLiveAdapter implements MathEditorAdapter {
  expressionFromEditorValue(value: string): MathExpression {
    if (!value.trim()) return { kind: "empty" };
    const statement = parseMathStatement(value);
    if (statement.kind !== "expression")
      throw new SyntaxError(
        "Enter the expression separately from its variable definition",
      );
    return statement.expression;
  }

  expressionToEditorValue(expression: MathExpression): string {
    return toLatex(expression);
  }

  async mount(
    host: HTMLElement,
    expression: MathExpression,
    callbacks: MathEditorCallbacks,
  ): Promise<() => void> {
    const { MathfieldElement } = await import("mathlive");
    const field = new MathfieldElement();
    host.replaceChildren(field);
    field.value = this.expressionToEditorValue(expression);
    field.mathVirtualKeyboardPolicy = "manual";
    field.inlineShortcuts = { ...field.inlineShortcuts, ...greekShortcuts };

    const input = () => {
      try {
        callbacks.onExpression(
          this.expressionFromEditorValue(field.getValue("ascii-math")),
        );
      } catch (error) {
        callbacks.onInvalid(
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Unsupported mathematical structure",
        );
      }
    };
    const keydown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === '"' && !field.getValue("ascii-math").trim()) {
        event.preventDefault();
        callbacks.onRequestText();
      }
    };
    field.addEventListener("input", input);
    field.addEventListener("keydown", keydown);

    const handle: MathEditorHandle = {
      focus: () => field.focus(),
      insert: (latex) =>
        field.insert(latex, {
          format: "latex",
          selectionMode: "placeholder",
          focus: true,
        }),
    };
    callbacks.onReady(handle);

    return () => {
      field.removeEventListener("input", input);
      field.removeEventListener("keydown", keydown);
      callbacks.onReady(null);
      field.remove();
    };
  }
}

function toLatex(expression: MathExpression): string {
  switch (expression.kind) {
    case "empty":
      return "";
    case "number":
      return String(expression.value);
    case "identifier":
      return (
        greekNames[expression.name] ??
        `\\mathrm{${escapeLatexText(expression.name)}}`
      );
    case "constant":
      return expression.name === "pi" ? "\\pi" : "e";
    case "quantity":
      return `${toLatex(expression.magnitude)}\\,\\mathrm{${escapeLatexText(expression.unit)}}`;
    case "unary":
      return `${expression.operator === "negative" ? "-" : "+"}${toLatex(expression.operand)}`;
    case "binary":
      return `${toLatex(expression.left)} ${binarySymbols[expression.operator]} ${toLatex(expression.right)}`;
    case "fraction":
      return `\\frac{${toLatex(expression.numerator)}}{${toLatex(expression.denominator)}}`;
    case "root":
      return `\\sqrt{${toLatex(expression.radicand)}}`;
    case "power":
      return `${group(expression.base)}^{${toLatex(expression.exponent)}}`;
    case "subscript":
      return `${group(expression.base)}_{${toLatex(expression.subscript)}}`;
    case "function":
      return `\\${expression.name}\\left(${toLatex(expression.argument)}\\right)`;
  }
}

const binarySymbols: Record<
  "add" | "subtract" | "multiply" | "divide" | "power",
  string
> = {
  add: "+",
  subtract: "-",
  multiply: "\\cdot",
  divide: "/",
  power: "^",
};

function group(expression: MathExpression): string {
  if (
    expression.kind === "identifier" ||
    expression.kind === "constant" ||
    expression.kind === "number"
  ) {
    return toLatex(expression);
  }
  return `\\left(${toLatex(expression)}\\right)`;
}

function escapeLatexText(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[{}\\]/g, "\\$&");
}


===== ./features/editor/math/mathlive-editor.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { MathExpression } from "../../../domain/expressions/ast";
import type { MathEditorHandle } from "./editor-port";
import { MathLiveAdapter } from "./mathlive-adapter";

const adapter = new MathLiveAdapter();

type Props = {
  expression: MathExpression;
  onChange(expression: MathExpression): void;
  onRequestText(): void;
  onReady(handle: MathEditorHandle | null): void;
};

export function MathLiveEditor({
  expression,
  onChange,
  onRequestText,
  onReady,
}: Props) {
  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const currentCallbacks = useRef({ onChange, onRequestText, onReady });
  const initialExpression = useRef(expression);
  const lastEmitted = useRef("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const serializedExpression = JSON.stringify(expression);

  currentCallbacks.current = { onChange, onRequestText, onReady };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!host.current) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    let dispose: (() => void) | undefined;

    void adapter
      .mount(host.current, initialExpression.current, {
        onExpression(next) {
          lastEmitted.current = JSON.stringify(next);
          setError("");
          currentCallbacks.current.onChange(next);
        },
        onInvalid: setError,
        onRequestText() {
          currentCallbacks.current.onRequestText();
        },
        onReady(handle) {
          currentCallbacks.current.onReady(handle);
        },
      })
      .then((cleanup) => {
        if (cancelled) cleanup();
        else dispose = cleanup;
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      dispose?.();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !host.current ||
      !host.current.firstElementChild ||
      lastEmitted.current === serializedExpression
    )
      return;
    const field = host.current.firstElementChild as HTMLElement & {
      value: string;
    };
    field.value = adapter.expressionToEditorValue(expression);
    lastEmitted.current = serializedExpression;
  }, [expression, serializedExpression]);

  return (
    <div className="math-editor-shell">
      <div
        className="mathlive-host"
        ref={host}
        aria-label="Mathematical expression editor"
      />
      {error && (
        <span className="inline-error" role="status">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== ./features/editor/math/editor-port.ts =====
import type { MathExpression } from "../../../domain/expressions/ast";

export type MathEditorHandle = {
  focus(): void;
  insert(latex: string): void;
};

export type MathEditorCallbacks = {
  onExpression(expression: MathExpression): void;
  onInvalid(message: string): void;
  onRequestText(): void;
  onReady(handle: MathEditorHandle | null): void;
};

export interface MathEditorAdapter {
  expressionFromEditorValue(value: string): MathExpression;
  expressionToEditorValue(expression: MathExpression): string;
  mount(
    host: HTMLElement,
    expression: MathExpression,
    callbacks: MathEditorCallbacks,
  ): Promise<() => void>;
}


===== ./features/editor/math/mathlive-adapter.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../../../domain/expressions/parser";
import { MathLiveAdapter } from "./mathlive-adapter";

describe("MathLive adapter", () => {
  const adapter = new MathLiveAdapter();

  it("converts supported editor output to Forma's structured expression tree", () => {
    expect(adapter.expressionFromEditorValue("((load * arm) / 2)")).toEqual({
      kind: "fraction",
      numerator: {
        kind: "binary",
        operator: "multiply",
        left: { kind: "identifier", name: "load" },
        right: { kind: "identifier", name: "arm" },
      },
      denominator: { kind: "number", value: 2 },
    });
  });

  it("reconstructs editable visual notation from the domain AST", () => {
    const statement = parseMathStatement("shear := torque * radius / polar");
    if (statement.kind !== "assignment")
      throw new Error("Expected assignment fixture");
    expect(adapter.expressionToEditorValue(statement.expression)).toBe(
      "\\frac{\\mathrm{torque} \\cdot \\mathrm{radius}}{\\mathrm{polar}}",
    );
  });

  it("does not accept editor markup or assignment syntax as an expression", () => {
    expect(() =>
      adapter.expressionFromEditorValue("\\htmlData{onclick}{x}"),
    ).toThrow();
    expect(() => adapter.expressionFromEditorValue("load := 2")).toThrow(
      "separately",
    );
  });

  it("round-trips an empty editable region without inserting a fake zero", () => {
    expect(adapter.expressionFromEditorValue("")).toEqual({ kind: "empty" });
    expect(adapter.expressionToEditorValue({ kind: "empty" })).toBe("");
  });
});


===== ./features/editor/state/editor-state.ts =====
export type EditorState = {
  activeRegionId: string | null;
  zoom: number;
  pan: { x: number; y: number };
  openPanel: "tools" | "document" | null;
};

export class EditorStateStore {
  private state: EditorState = {
    activeRegionId: null,
    zoom: 1,
    pan: { x: 0, y: 0 },
    openPanel: "tools",
  };
  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  getSnapshot = (): EditorState => this.state;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };

  update(updater: (state: EditorState) => EditorState): void {
    this.state = updater(this.state);
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
}

===== ./tests/e2e/worksheet.spec.ts =====
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import type { WorksheetApiRecord } from "../../shared/contracts/worksheet-api";

const password = "Forma-Playwright-Only-2026!";

test("an authenticated user edits, saves, and reopens a calculation worksheet", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await register(page);
  await expect(page.locator("math-field")).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(page.getByText("397.1565", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  await replaceMathValue(page, 0, "160");
  await expect(page.getByText("446.4", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect
    .poll(async () => (await worksheetRecords(page))[0]?.revision)
    .toBe(2);

  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.locator("math-field")).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(page.getByText("446.4", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  expect(
    (await worksheetRecords(page))[0].document.regions.find(
      (region) => region.id === "load",
    ),
  ).toMatchObject({
    content: {
      kind: "assignment",
      name: "load",
      expression: { kind: "number", value: 160 },
    },
  });
});

test("a user can create and switch between worksheets", async ({ page }) => {
  await register(page);
  const first = (await worksheetRecords(page))[0];

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New worksheet" }).click();
  const title = page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Worksheet name" });
  await expect(title).toHaveValue("Untitled worksheet");
  await title.fill("Beam calculation");
  await expect.poll(async () => (await worksheetRecords(page)).length).toBe(2);

  const picker = page.getByLabel("Open worksheet");
  await picker.selectOption(first.document.id);
  await expect(title).toHaveValue("Shaft fatigue worksheet");
  await picker.selectOption(
    (await worksheetRecords(page)).find(
      (record) => record.document.title === "Beam calculation",
    )!.document.id,
  );
  await expect(title).toHaveValue("Beam calculation");
  await expect(page.locator("math-field")).toHaveCount(3);
});

test("worksheet sharing distinguishes viewers from editors and hides unshared documents", async ({
  browser,
  page,
}) => {
  await register(page);
  const ownerDocument = (await worksheetRecords(page))[0];
  const ownerId = await sessionUserId(page);

  const viewerContext = await browser.newContext();
  const viewerPage = await viewerContext.newPage();
  await register(viewerPage);
  const viewerId = await sessionUserId(viewerPage);
  const worksheetPath = `/api/worksheets/${ownerDocument.document.id}`;

  const hidden = await viewerContext.request.get(worksheetPath);
  expect(hidden.status()).toBe(404);

  const grantViewer = await page
    .context()
    .request.post(`${worksheetPath}/permissions`, {
      data: { userId: viewerId, permission: "viewer" },
    });
  expect(grantViewer.status()).toBe(200);
  expect((await viewerContext.request.get(worksheetPath)).status()).toBe(200);

  const viewerEdit = await viewerContext.request.put(worksheetPath, {
    data: {
      document: { ...ownerDocument.document, title: "Unauthorized edit" },
      baseRevision: 1,
      requestId: crypto.randomUUID(),
    },
  });
  expect(viewerEdit.status()).toBe(403);

  const grantEditor = await page
    .context()
    .request.post(`${worksheetPath}/permissions`, {
      data: { userId: viewerId, permission: "editor" },
    });
  expect(grantEditor.status()).toBe(200);
  const editorEdit = await viewerContext.request.put(worksheetPath, {
    data: {
      document: { ...ownerDocument.document, title: "Editor update" },
      baseRevision: 1,
      requestId: crypto.randomUUID(),
    },
  });
  expect(editorEdit.status()).toBe(200);
  expect((await worksheetRecords(page))[0].document.title).toBe(
    "Editor update",
  );
  expect(ownerId).not.toBe(viewerId);
  await viewerContext.close();
});

test("stale concurrent edits are surfaced and recoverable as a copy", async ({
  browser,
  page,
}) => {
  const account = await register(page);
  const otherContext = await browser.newContext();
  const otherPage = await otherContext.newPage();
  await signIn(otherPage, account.email);
  await expect(page.locator("math-field")).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(otherPage.locator("math-field")).toHaveCount(3);

  await replaceMathValue(otherPage, 0, "150");
  await expect(otherPage.getByText("418.5", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect
    .poll(async () => (await worksheetRecords(otherPage))[0]?.revision)
    .toBe(2);

  await replaceMathValue(page, 0, "160");
  await expect(page.getByText("446.4", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText("Newer server revision detected.")).toBeVisible();
  expect(
    (await worksheetRecords(page))[0].document.regions.find(
      (region) => region.id === "load",
    ),
  ).toMatchObject({
    content: { expression: { kind: "number", value: 150 } },
  });

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save draft as copy" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("Recovered as a new worksheet")).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(async () => (await worksheetRecords(page)).length).toBe(2);
  await otherContext.close();
});

async function register(page: Page): Promise<{ email: string }> {
  const email = `forma-${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.invalid`;
  await page.goto("/sign-in");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /New to Forma/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Name").fill("Forma E2E User");
  await page.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click();
  await page.waitForURL("/");
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("region", { name: "Worksheet canvas" }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator("math-field").first()).toBeVisible();
  await expect
    .poll(async () => (await worksheetRecords(page)).length)
    .toBeGreaterThan(0);
  return { email };
}

async function replaceMathValue(page: Page, index: number, value: string) {
  const field = page.locator("math-field").nth(index);
  await field.click();
  await field.evaluate((element) => {
    const mathfield = element as HTMLElement & {
      executeCommand(command: string): boolean;
      focus(): void;
    };
    mathfield.focus();
    mathfield.executeCommand("selectAll");
  });
  await field.pressSequentially(value);
  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      field.evaluate((element) =>
        (
          element as HTMLElement & { getValue(format: string): string }
        ).getValue("ascii-math"),
      ),
    )
    .toBe(value);
  return field;
}

async function signIn(page: Page, email: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto("/sign-in");
  await page.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
  await page.waitForURL("/");
}

async function worksheetRecords(page: Page): Promise<WorksheetApiRecord[]> {
  const response = await page.context().request.get("/api/worksheets");
  if (!response.ok())
    throw new Error(`Worksheet list returned ${response.status()}`);
  const payload = (await response.json()) as { data: WorksheetApiRecord[] };
  return payload.data;
}

async function sessionUserId(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  const response = await page.context().request.get("/api/auth/get-session");
  expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
  const payload = (await response.json()) as { user: { id: string } };
  return payload.user.id;
}


===== ./eslint.config.mjs =====
import { FlatCompat } from "@eslint/eslintrc";
import { dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const compat = new FlatCompat({ baseDirectory: dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) });

===== ./shared/contracts/worksheet-api.ts =====
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "../../domain/worksheet/model";

export type WorksheetAccess = "owner" | "editor" | "viewer";

export type WorksheetApiRecord = {
  document: WorksheetDocument;
  ownerId: string;
  permission: WorksheetAccess;
  revision: number;
  updatedAt: string;
};

export type ApiSuccess<T> = { data: T };
export type ApiFailure = {
  error: {
    code: string;
    message: string;
    requestId: string;
    details?: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
};


===== ./next.config.ts =====
import type { NextConfig } from "next";

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  output: "standalone",
  outputFileTracingRoot: process.cwd(),
  async headers() {
    const development = process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production";
    const contentSecurityPolicy = [
      "default-src 'self'",
      `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'${development ? " 'unsafe-eval'" : ""}`,
      "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
      "font-src 'self' data:",
      "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
      `connect-src 'self'${development ? " ws: wss:" : ""}`,
      "object-src 'none'",
      "base-uri 'self'",
      "form-action 'self'",
      "frame-ancestors 'none'",
      "upgrade-insecure-requests",
    ].join("; ");

    return [
      {
        source: "/:path*",
        headers: [
          { key: "Content-Security-Policy", value: contentSecurityPolicy },
          { key: "X-Content-Type-Options", value: "nosniff" },
          { key: "X-Frame-Options", value: "DENY" },
          { key: "Referrer-Policy", value: "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" },
          {
            key: "Permissions-Policy",
            value: "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()",
          },
          {
            key: "Strict-Transport-Security",
            value: "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains",
          },
        ],
      },
    ];
  },
};

export default nextConfig;
