===== ./app/page.tsx =====
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { currentSession } from "../server/auth/session";
import { WorksheetWorkspace } from "../features/worksheets/components/worksheet-workspace";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function Home() {
  const session = await currentSession();
  if (!session) redirect("/sign-in");
  return <WorksheetWorkspace userId={session.user.id} />;
}


===== ./app/api/worksheets/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { sessionFromRequest } from "../../../server/auth/session";
import {
  errorResponse,
  isRecord,
  readJsonBody,
  requestIdFor,
  responseHeaders,
} from "../../../server/api/http";
import { allowWrite } from "../../../server/api/rate-limit";
import { getWorksheetService } from "../../../server/services/container";
import { WorksheetApplicationError } from "../../../server/services/worksheet-service";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    const records = await getWorksheetService().list(session.user.id);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { data: records },
      { headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    if (!allowWrite(`create:${session.user.id}`, 20)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: {
            code: "rate_limited",
            message: "Too many requests",
            requestId,
          },
        },
        { status: 429, headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
      );
    }
    const body = await readJsonBody(request);
    if (!isRecord(body) || !("document" in body))
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "A worksheet document is required",
      );
    const record = await getWorksheetService().create(
      session.user.id,
      body.document,
    );
    return NextResponse.json(
      { data: record },
      { status: 201, headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}


===== ./app/api/worksheets/[id]/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { sessionFromRequest } from "../../../../server/auth/session";
import {
  errorResponse,
  isRecord,
  readJsonBody,
  requestIdFor,
  responseHeaders,
} from "../../../../server/api/http";
import { allowWrite } from "../../../../server/api/rate-limit";
import { getWorksheetService } from "../../../../server/services/container";
import { WorksheetApplicationError } from "../../../../server/services/worksheet-service";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
type Context = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function GET(request: Request, context: Context) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const record = await getWorksheetService().get(id, session.user.id);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { data: record },
      { headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}

export async function PUT(request: Request, context: Context) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    if (!allowWrite(`save:${session.user.id}`, 120)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: {
            code: "rate_limited",
            message: "Too many requests",
            requestId,
          },
        },
        { status: 429, headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
      );
    }
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const body = await readJsonBody(request);
    if (
      !isRecord(body) ||
      !("document" in body) ||
      typeof body.baseRevision !== "number" ||
      typeof body.requestId !== "string"
    ) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "Document, baseRevision, and requestId are required",
      );
    }
    const record = await getWorksheetService().save(
      id,
      session.user.id,
      body.baseRevision,
      body.requestId,
      body.document,
    );
    return NextResponse.json(
      { data: record },
      { headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request, context: Context) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    const { id } = await context.params;
    await getWorksheetService().delete(id, session.user.id);
    return new NextResponse(null, {
      status: 204,
      headers: responseHeaders(requestId),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}


===== ./app/api/worksheets/[id]/permissions/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { sessionFromRequest } from "../../../../../server/auth/session";
import {
  errorResponse,
  isRecord,
  readJsonBody,
  requestIdFor,
  responseHeaders,
} from "../../../../../server/api/http";
import { getWorksheetService } from "../../../../../server/services/container";
import { WorksheetApplicationError } from "../../../../../server/services/worksheet-service";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function POST(
  request: Request,
  context: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const body = await readJsonBody(request, 16_384);
    if (
      !isRecord(body) ||
      typeof body.userId !== "string" ||
      (body.permission !== "viewer" && body.permission !== "editor")
    ) {
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        400,
        "invalid_document",
        "A userId and viewer/editor permission are required",
      );
    }
    await getWorksheetService().grantPermission(
      id,
      session.user.id,
      body.userId,
      body.permission,
    );
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        data: {
          worksheetId: id,
          userId: body.userId,
          permission: body.permission,
        },
      },
      { headers: responseHeaders(requestId) },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}


===== ./app/api/worksheets/[id]/revisions/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { sessionFromRequest } from "../../../../../server/auth/session";
import {
  errorResponse,
  requestIdFor,
  responseHeaders,
} from "../../../../../server/api/http";

import { WorksheetApplicationError } from "../../../../../server/services/worksheet-service";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function GET(
  request: Request,
  context: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    const session = await sessionFromRequest(request);
    if (!session)
      throw new WorksheetApplicationError(
        401,
        "unauthenticated",
        "Authentication is required",
      );

      { data: revisions },

    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error, requestId);
  }
}


===== ./app/api/health/ready/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { getDatabase } from "../../../../server/database/client";
import { logServerError } from "../../../../server/observability/logger";
import { requestIdFor } from "../../../../server/api/http";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const requestId = requestIdFor(request);
  try {
    await getDatabase().execute(sql`select 1`);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { status: "ok", database: "ok", requestId },
      { headers: { "X-Request-Id": requestId, "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    logServerError("health.database_unavailable", requestId, error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { status: "unavailable", database: "unavailable", requestId },
      {
        status: 503,
        headers: { "X-Request-Id": requestId, "Cache-Control": "no-store" },
      },
    );
  }
}

===== ./scripts/standalone.mjs =====
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { cp, mkdir } from "node:fs/promises";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve } from "node:path";

export async function prepareStandalone(root = process.cwd()) {
  const serverPath = resolve(root, ".next/standalone/server.js");
  if (!existsSync(serverPath)) {
    throw new Error("Standalone output is missing; run npm run build first");
  }

  const standaloneNext = resolve(root, ".next/standalone/.next");
  await mkdir(resolve(standaloneNext, "static"), { recursive: true });
  await cp(resolve(root, ".next/static"), resolve(standaloneNext, "static"), {
    recursive: true,
    force: true,
  });

  if (existsSync(resolve(root, "public"))) {
    await cp(
      resolve(root, "public"),
      resolve(root, ".next/standalone/public"),
      {
        recursive: true,
        force: true,
      },
    );
  }
}

export function startStandalone({
  root = process.cwd(),
  hostname = "0.0.0.0",
  port = "3000",
} = {}) {
  const serverPath = resolve(root, ".next/standalone/server.js");
  const server = spawn(process.execPath, [serverPath], {
    cwd: root,
    stdio: "inherit",
    env: { ...process.env, HOSTNAME: hostname, PORT: port },
  });

  for (const signal of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"]) {
    process.on(signal, () => server.kill(signal));
  }

  server.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
    process.exitCode = signal ? 1 : (code ?? 1);
  });

  return server;
}


===== ./scripts/start-e2e-server.mjs =====
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { prepareStandalone, startStandalone } from "./standalone.mjs";

const root = process.cwd();
const serverPath = resolve(root, ".next/standalone/server.js");

if (!existsSync(serverPath) || process.env.CI !== "true") {
  await run(process.platform === "win32" ? "npm.cmd" : "npm", ["run", "build"]);
}

await prepareStandalone(root);
startStandalone({ root, hostname: "127.0.0.1", port: "3001" });

function run(command, args) {
  return new Promise((resolveRun, rejectRun) => {
    const processHandle = spawn(command, args, {
      cwd: root,
      stdio: "inherit",
      env: process.env,
    });
    processHandle.on("error", rejectRun);
    processHandle.on("exit", (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolveRun();
      else rejectRun(new Error(`Command failed: ${command} ${args.join(" ")}`));
    });
  });
}

===== ./domain/expressions/parser.ts =====
import type {
  BinaryOperator,
  FunctionName,
  MathExpression,
  MathStatement,
} from "./ast";

type Token = { type: "number" | "name" | "operator"; value: string };

const functionNames = new Set<FunctionName>([
  "abs",
  "cos",
  "exp",
  "ln",
  "log",
  "sin",
  "sqrt",
  "tan",
]);

function tokenize(source: string): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];
  let index = 0;

  while (index < source.length) {
    const rest = source.slice(index);
    const whitespace = rest.match(/^\s+/);
    if (whitespace) {
      index += whitespace[0].length;
      continue;
    }

    const number = rest.match(/^(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?/);
    if (number) {
      tokens.push({ type: "number", value: number[0] });
      index += number[0].length;
      continue;
    }

    const name = rest.match(/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*/);
    if (name) {
      tokens.push({ type: "name", value: name[0] });
      index += name[0].length;
      continue;
    }

    const operator = rest.match(/^(?::=|[+*/^(),-])/);
    if (operator) {
      tokens.push({ type: "operator", value: operator[0] });
      index += operator[0].length;
      continue;
    }

    throw new SyntaxError(`Unexpected character "${source[index]}"`);
  }

  return tokens;
}

export function parseMathStatement(source: string): MathStatement {
  if (!source.trim())
    return { kind: "expression", expression: { kind: "empty" } };
  const tokens = tokenize(source);
  let position = 0;

  function peek(value?: string): Token | undefined {
    const token = tokens[position];
    return value === undefined || token?.value === value ? token : undefined;
  }

  function take(value?: string): Token {
    const token = peek(value);
    if (!token)
      throw new SyntaxError(
        value ? `Expected "${value}"` : "Unexpected end of expression",
      );
    position += 1;
    return token;
  }

  function primary(): MathExpression {
    const token = take();
    if (token.type === "number") {
      const value = Number(token.value);
      if (!Number.isFinite(value))
        throw new SyntaxError("Numeric literal is not finite");
      return { kind: "number", value };
    }

    if (token.value === "(") {
      const expression = addSub();
      take(")");
      return expression;
    }

    if (token.type === "name") {
      const normalized = token.value.toLowerCase();
      if (peek("(")) {
        take("(");
        const argument = addSub();
        take(")");
        if (!functionNames.has(normalized as FunctionName)) {
          throw new SyntaxError(`Unknown function "${token.value}"`);
        }
        if (normalized === "sqrt") return { kind: "root", radicand: argument };
        return { kind: "function", name: normalized as FunctionName, argument };
      }
      if (normalized === "pi" || normalized === "e")
        return { kind: "constant", name: normalized };
      return { kind: "identifier", name: token.value };
    }

    throw new SyntaxError(`Unexpected token "${token.value}"`);
  }

  function power(): MathExpression {
    const base = primary();
    if (!peek("^")) return base;
    take("^");
    return { kind: "power", base, exponent: unary() };
  }

  function unary(): MathExpression {
    if (peek("+")) {
      take("+");
      return { kind: "unary", operator: "positive", operand: unary() };
    }
    if (peek("-")) {
      take("-");
      return { kind: "unary", operator: "negative", operand: unary() };
    }
    return power();
  }

  function mulDiv(): MathExpression {
    let expression = unary();
    while (peek("*") || peek("/")) {
      const operator = take().value === "*" ? "multiply" : "divide";
      const right = unary();
      expression =
        operator === "divide"
          ? { kind: "fraction", numerator: expression, denominator: right }
          : { kind: "binary", operator, left: expression, right };
    }
    return expression;
  }

  function addSub(): MathExpression {
    let expression = mulDiv();
    while (peek("+") || peek("-")) {
      const operator: BinaryOperator =
        take().value === "+" ? "add" : "subtract";
      expression = {
        kind: "binary",
        operator,
        left: expression,
        right: mulDiv(),
      };
    }
    return expression;
  }

  let statement: MathStatement;
  if (tokens[0]?.type === "name" && tokens[1]?.value === ":=") {
    const name = take().value;
    take(":=");
    statement = { kind: "assignment", name, expression: addSub() };
  } else {
    statement = { kind: "expression", expression: addSub() };
  }

  if (position !== tokens.length)
    throw new SyntaxError(`Unexpected token "${tokens[position].value}"`);
  return statement;
}


===== ./domain/expressions/parser.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseMathStatement } from "./parser";

describe("math expression parser", () => {
  it("preserves assignment and operator precedence in structured nodes", () => {
    expect(parseMathStatement("stress := load * arm / 2")).toEqual({
      kind: "assignment",
      name: "stress",
      expression: {
        kind: "fraction",
        numerator: {
          kind: "binary",
          operator: "multiply",
          left: { kind: "identifier", name: "load" },
          right: { kind: "identifier", name: "arm" },
        },
        denominator: { kind: "number", value: 2 },
      },
    });
  });

  it("maps square roots to a structural root node", () => {
    expect(parseMathStatement("sqrt(pi)")).toEqual({
      kind: "expression",
      expression: { kind: "root", radicand: { kind: "constant", name: "pi" } },
    });
  });

  it("rejects unsupported syntax without executing it", () => {
    expect(() => parseMathStatement("globalThis.process.exit()")).toThrow();
  });
});


===== ./domain/expressions/ast.ts =====
export type BinaryOperator =
  "add" | "subtract" | "multiply" | "divide" | "power";
export type UnaryOperator = "positive" | "negative";
export type FunctionName =
  "abs" | "cos" | "exp" | "ln" | "log" | "sin" | "sqrt" | "tan";

export type MathExpression =
  | { kind: "empty" }
  | { kind: "number"; value: number }
  | { kind: "identifier"; name: string }
  | { kind: "constant"; name: "pi" | "e" }
  | { kind: "quantity"; magnitude: MathExpression; unit: string }
  | { kind: "unary"; operator: UnaryOperator; operand: MathExpression }
  | {
      kind: "binary";
      operator: BinaryOperator;
      left: MathExpression;
      right: MathExpression;
    }
  | { kind: "function"; name: FunctionName; argument: MathExpression }
  | { kind: "fraction"; numerator: MathExpression; denominator: MathExpression }
  | { kind: "root"; radicand: MathExpression }
  | { kind: "power"; base: MathExpression; exponent: MathExpression }
  | { kind: "subscript"; base: MathExpression; subscript: MathExpression };

export type MathStatement =
  | { kind: "expression"; expression: MathExpression }
  | { kind: "assignment"; name: string; expression: MathExpression };


===== ./domain/calculation/types.ts =====
export type CalculationErrorCode =
  | "invalid-expression"
  | "undefined-variable"
  | "circular-dependency"
  | "duplicate-definition"
  | "division-by-zero"
  | "numerical-failure"
  | "unsupported-operation"
  | "dependency-failed";

export type RegionCalculationResult =
  | { status: "success"; value: number }
  | { status: "error"; code: CalculationErrorCode; message: string }
  | { status: "pending" };

export type CalculationResult = {
  regions: Record<string, RegionCalculationResult>;
  variables: Record<string, number>;
  dependencies: Record<string, string[]>;
  evaluationOrder: string[];
  evaluatedRegionIds: string[];
};


===== ./domain/calculation/engine.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../expressions/parser";
import { DEFAULT_PAGE, type WorksheetDocument } from "../worksheet/model";
import { calculateWorksheet, CalculationSession } from "./engine";

function worksheet(expressions: string[]): WorksheetDocument {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    id: "calc-test",
    title: "Calculation test",
    settings: { page: DEFAULT_PAGE, grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 } },
    regions: expressions.map((content, index) => ({
      id: `math-${index}`,
      type: "math" as const,
      content: parseMathStatement(content),
      position: { x: 10, y: 10 + index * 10 },
      zIndex: index,
    })),
  };
}

describe("calculation engine", () => {
  it("resolves definitions independent of spatial order", () => {
    const result = calculateWorksheet(
      worksheet(["moment := load * arm", "load := 142.35", "arm := 2.79"]),
    );
    expect(result.regions["math-0"]).toEqual({
      status: "success",
      value: 397.1565,
    });
    expect(result.dependencies["math-0"]).toEqual(["math-1", "math-2"]);
  });

  it("detects cycles and undefined variables with structured errors", () => {
    const result = calculateWorksheet(
      worksheet(["a := b + 1", "b := a + 1", "c := missing + 2"]),
    );
    expect(result.regions["math-0"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "circular-dependency",
    });
    expect(result.regions["math-1"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "circular-dependency",
    });
    expect(result.regions["math-2"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "undefined-variable",
    });
  });

  it("rejects duplicate definitions rather than silently choosing one", () => {
    const result = calculateWorksheet(
      worksheet(["load := 4", "load := 5", "result := load * 2"]),
    );
    expect(result.regions["math-0"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "duplicate-definition",
    });
    expect(result.regions["math-1"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "duplicate-definition",
    });
    expect(result.regions["math-2"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "duplicate-definition",
    });
  });

  it("reports division by zero and non-real numerical results", () => {
    const result = calculateWorksheet(worksheet(["1 / 0", "sqrt(-1)"]));
    expect(result.regions["math-0"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "division-by-zero",
    });
    expect(result.regions["math-1"]).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      code: "numerical-failure",
    });
  });

  it("calculates a shaft torque and solid-shaft torsional stress", () => {
    const result = calculateWorksheet(
      worksheet([
        "force := 1200",
        "arm := 0.35",
        "torque := force * arm",
        "diameter := 0.02",
        "polar := pi * diameter^4 / 32",
        "shear := torque * (diameter / 2) / polar",
      ]),
    );
    expect(result.variables.torque).toBe(420);
    expect(result.variables.shear).toBeCloseTo(267380304.394, 2);
  });

  it("recalculates only changed regions and their transitive dependents", () => {
    const session = new CalculationSession();
    const original = worksheet([
      "load := 142.35",
      "arm := 2.79",
      "moment := load * arm",
    ]);
    session.calculate(original);
    const changed: WorksheetDocument = {
      ...original,
      regions: original.regions.map((region) =>
        region.id === "math-0" && region.type === "math"
          ? { ...region, content: parseMathStatement("load := 150") }
          : region,
      ),
    };

    const result = session.calculate(changed);
    expect(result.variables.moment).toBeCloseTo(418.5);
    expect(new Set(result.evaluatedRegionIds)).toEqual(
      new Set(["math-0", "math-2"]),
    );

    expect(session.calculate(changed).evaluatedRegionIds).toEqual([]);
  });
});


===== ./domain/calculation/engine.ts =====
import type { FunctionName, MathExpression } from "../expressions/ast";
import type { MathRegion, WorksheetDocument } from "../worksheet/model";
import type {
  CalculationErrorCode,
  CalculationResult,
  RegionCalculationResult,
} from "./types";

class CalculationFailure extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: CalculationErrorCode,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

type Definition = { region: MathRegion; name: string };

export function calculateWorksheet(
  document: WorksheetDocument,
): CalculationResult {
  return calculateWithPrevious(document);
}

export class CalculationSession {
  private document: WorksheetDocument | null = null;
  private result: CalculationResult | null = null;

  calculate(document: WorksheetDocument): CalculationResult {
    this.result = calculateWithPrevious(document, this.document, this.result);
    this.document = document;
    return this.result;
  }
}

function calculateWithPrevious(
  document: WorksheetDocument,
  previousDocument?: WorksheetDocument | null,
  previousResult?: CalculationResult | null,
): CalculationResult {
  const mathRegions = document.regions.filter(
    (region): region is MathRegion => region.type === "math",
  );
  const definitions = new Map<string, Definition[]>();
  const resultById: Record<string, RegionCalculationResult> = {};
  const variables: Record<string, number> = {};
  const changedIds =
    previousDocument && previousResult
      ? changedMathRegionIds(previousDocument, document)
      : new Set(mathRegions.map((region) => region.id));

  for (const region of mathRegions) {
    if (
      region.content.kind === "expression" &&
      region.content.expression.kind === "empty"
    ) {
      resultById[region.id] = { status: "pending" };
      continue;
    }
    if (region.content.kind !== "assignment") continue;
    const current = definitions.get(region.content.name) ?? [];
    current.push({ region, name: region.content.name });
    definitions.set(region.content.name, current);
  }

  const uniqueDefinitions = new Map<string, Definition>();
  const dependencies: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const [name, entries] of definitions) {
    if (entries.length > 1) {
      for (const { region } of entries) {
        resultById[region.id] = error(
          "duplicate-definition",
          `"${name}" is defined more than once`,
        );
      }
    } else {
      uniqueDefinitions.set(name, entries[0]);
    }
  }
  const definitionsById = new Map(
    [...uniqueDefinitions.values()].map((definition) => [
      definition.region.id,
      definition,
    ]),
  );

  for (const region of mathRegions) {
    const expression = region.content.expression;
    const names = collectIdentifiers(expression);
    dependencies[region.id] = [
      ...new Set(
        names.flatMap((name) => {
          const definition = uniqueDefinitions.get(name);
          return definition ? [definition.region.id] : [];
        }),
      ),
    ];
  }

  const invalidated =
    previousDocument && previousResult
      ? dependentClosure(
          changedIds,
          previousDocument,
          document,
          previousResult.dependencies,
          dependencies,
        )
      : new Set(mathRegions.map((region) => region.id));

  if (previousResult) {
    for (const region of mathRegions) {
      const previous = previousResult.regions[region.id];
      if (!invalidated.has(region.id) && previous)
        resultById[region.id] = previous;
    }
  }

  for (const [name, entries] of definitions) {
    if (entries.length !== 1) continue;
    const definition = entries[0];
    const previous = resultById[definition.region.id];
    if (previous?.status === "success") variables[name] = previous.value;
  }

  const cyclic = findCycles(uniqueDefinitions, dependencies);
  const evaluationOrder = topologicalOrder(
    uniqueDefinitions,
    dependencies,
    cyclic,
  );
  const evaluatedRegionIds: string[] = [];

  for (const id of cyclic)
    resultById[id] = error(
      "circular-dependency",
      "Circular variable definition",
    );

  for (const id of evaluationOrder) {
    const definition = definitionsById.get(id);
    if (!definition || resultById[id]) continue;
    if (!invalidated.has(id)) continue;

    const failedDependency = dependencies[id].find(
      (dependencyId) => resultById[dependencyId]?.status === "error",
    );
    if (failedDependency) {
      resultById[id] = error(
        "dependency-failed",
        "A referenced calculation did not succeed",
      );
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const value = evaluateExpression(
        definition.region.content.expression,
        variables,
        definitions,
      );
      variables[definition.name] = value;
      resultById[id] = { status: "success", value };
    } catch (failure) {
      resultById[id] = toResult(failure);
    }
    evaluatedRegionIds.push(id);
  }

  for (const region of mathRegions) {
    if (
      region.content.kind !== "expression" ||
      (resultById[region.id] && !invalidated.has(region.id))
    )
      continue;
    if (region.content.expression.kind === "empty") {
      resultById[region.id] = { status: "pending" };
      continue;
    }
    if (!invalidated.has(region.id)) continue;
    try {
      resultById[region.id] = {
        status: "success",
        value: evaluateExpression(
          region.content.expression,
          variables,
          definitions,
        ),
      };
    } catch (failure) {
      resultById[region.id] = toResult(failure);
    }
    evaluatedRegionIds.push(region.id);
  }

  return {
    regions: resultById,
    variables,
    dependencies,
    evaluationOrder,
    evaluatedRegionIds,
  };
}

function changedMathRegionIds(
  previous: WorksheetDocument,
  current: WorksheetDocument,
): Set<string> {
  const priorById = new Map(
    previous.regions
      .filter((region): region is MathRegion => region.type === "math")
      .map((region) => [region.id, region]),
  );
  const currentById = new Map(
    current.regions
      .filter((region): region is MathRegion => region.type === "math")
      .map((region) => [region.id, region]),
  );
  const changed = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of new Set([...priorById.keys(), ...currentById.keys()])) {
    if (
      JSON.stringify(priorById.get(id)) !== JSON.stringify(currentById.get(id))
    )
      changed.add(id);
  }
  return changed;
}

function dependentClosure(
  changedIds: Set<string>,
  previousDocument: WorksheetDocument,
  currentDocument: WorksheetDocument,
  previousDependencies: Record<string, string[]>,
  currentDependencies: Record<string, string[]>,
): Set<string> {
  const previousNames = assignmentNames(previousDocument);
  const currentNames = assignmentNames(currentDocument);
  const changedNames = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of changedIds) {
    const previousName = previousNames.get(id);
    const currentName = currentNames.get(id);
    if (previousName) changedNames.add(previousName);
    if (currentName) changedNames.add(currentName);
  }
  for (const [id, name] of previousNames)
    if (changedNames.has(name)) changedIds.add(id);
  for (const [id, name] of currentNames)
    if (changedNames.has(name)) changedIds.add(id);

  const reverseGraphs = [
    reverseDependencies(previousDependencies),
    reverseDependencies(currentDependencies),
  ];
  const invalidated = new Set(changedIds);
  const queue = [...changedIds];
  while (queue.length) {
    const dependencyId = queue.pop()!;
    for (const reverse of reverseGraphs) {
      for (const dependentId of reverse.get(dependencyId) ?? []) {
        if (!invalidated.has(dependentId)) {
          invalidated.add(dependentId);
          queue.push(dependentId);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return invalidated;
}

function assignmentNames(document: WorksheetDocument): Map<string, string> {
  return new Map(
    document.regions.flatMap((region) =>
      region.type === "math" && region.content.kind === "assignment"
        ? [[region.id, region.content.name] as const]
        : [],
    ),
  );
}

function reverseDependencies(
  dependencies: Record<string, string[]>,
): Map<string, string[]> {
  const reverse = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const [regionId, dependencyIds] of Object.entries(dependencies)) {
    for (const dependencyId of dependencyIds) {
      const dependents = reverse.get(dependencyId) ?? [];
      dependents.push(regionId);
      reverse.set(dependencyId, dependents);
    }
  }
  return reverse;
}

function evaluateExpression(
  expression: MathExpression,
  variables: Record<string, number>,
  definitions: Map<string, Definition[]>,
): number {
  let value: number;
  switch (expression.kind) {
    case "number":
      value = expression.value;
      break;
    case "empty":
      throw new CalculationFailure(
        "invalid-expression",
        "Enter a mathematical expression",
      );
    case "constant":
      value = expression.name === "pi" ? Math.PI : Math.E;
      break;
    case "quantity":
      throw new CalculationFailure(
        "unsupported-operation",
        "Quantity expressions require dimensional calculation support",
      );
    case "identifier": {
      const matches = definitions.get(expression.name);
      if (matches && matches.length > 1)
        throw new CalculationFailure(
          "duplicate-definition",
          `"${expression.name}" is defined more than once`,
        );
      if (!Object.hasOwn(variables, expression.name))
        throw new CalculationFailure(
          "undefined-variable",
          `"${expression.name}" is not defined`,
        );
      value = variables[expression.name];
      break;
    }
    case "unary": {
      const operand = evaluateExpression(
        expression.operand,
        variables,
        definitions,
      );
      value = expression.operator === "negative" ? -operand : operand;
      break;
    }
    case "binary": {
      const left = evaluateExpression(expression.left, variables, definitions);
      const right = evaluateExpression(
        expression.right,
        variables,
        definitions,
      );
      switch (expression.operator) {
        case "add":
          value = left + right;
          break;
        case "subtract":
          value = left - right;
          break;
        case "multiply":
          value = left * right;
          break;
        case "divide":
          value = divide(left, right);
          break;
        case "power":
          value = left ** right;
          break;
      }
      break;
    }
    case "fraction":
      value = divide(
        evaluateExpression(expression.numerator, variables, definitions),
        evaluateExpression(expression.denominator, variables, definitions),
      );
      break;
    case "power":
      value =
        evaluateExpression(expression.base, variables, definitions) **
        evaluateExpression(expression.exponent, variables, definitions);
      break;
    case "root":
      value = Math.sqrt(
        evaluateExpression(expression.radicand, variables, definitions),
      );
      break;
    case "function": {
      const argument = evaluateExpression(
        expression.argument,
        variables,
        definitions,
      );
      const functions: Record<FunctionName, (value: number) => number> = {
        abs: Math.abs,
        cos: Math.cos,
        exp: Math.exp,
        ln: Math.log,
        log: Math.log10,
        sin: Math.sin,
        sqrt: Math.sqrt,
        tan: Math.tan,
      };
      value = functions[expression.name](argument);
      break;
    }
    case "subscript":
      throw new CalculationFailure(
        "unsupported-operation",
        "Subscripted expressions are not yet numerically evaluable",
      );
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(value))
    throw new CalculationFailure(
      "numerical-failure",
      "The result is not a finite real number",
    );
  return value;
}

function divide(numerator: number, denominator: number): number {
  if (denominator === 0)
    throw new CalculationFailure("division-by-zero", "Division by zero");
  return numerator / denominator;
}

function collectIdentifiers(expression: MathExpression): string[] {
  switch (expression.kind) {
    case "identifier":
      return [expression.name];
    case "unary":
      return collectIdentifiers(expression.operand);
    case "binary":
      return [
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.left),
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.right),
      ];
    case "function":
      return collectIdentifiers(expression.argument);
    case "fraction":
      return [
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.numerator),
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.denominator),
      ];
    case "root":
      return collectIdentifiers(expression.radicand);
    case "power":
      return [
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.base),
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.exponent),
      ];
    case "subscript":
      return [
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.base),
        ...collectIdentifiers(expression.subscript),
      ];
    case "quantity":
      return collectIdentifiers(expression.magnitude);
    case "empty":
    case "number":
    case "constant":
      return [];
  }
}

function findCycles(
  definitions: Map<string, Definition>,
  dependencies: Record<string, string[]>,
): Set<string> {
  const colors = new Map<string, "visiting" | "complete">();
  const stack: string[] = [];
  const cyclic = new Set<string>();

  function visit(id: string): void {
    if (colors.get(id) === "complete") return;
    if (colors.get(id) === "visiting") {
      const start = stack.indexOf(id);
      for (const cycleId of stack.slice(start)) cyclic.add(cycleId);
      return;
    }
    colors.set(id, "visiting");
    stack.push(id);
    for (const dependencyId of dependencies[id] ?? []) {
      if (definitionsHasId(definitions, dependencyId)) visit(dependencyId);
    }
    stack.pop();
    colors.set(id, "complete");
  }

  for (const definition of definitions.values()) visit(definition.region.id);
  return cyclic;
}

function topologicalOrder(
  definitions: Map<string, Definition>,
  dependencies: Record<string, string[]>,
  cyclic: Set<string>,
): string[] {
  const order: string[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<string>();

  function visit(id: string): void {
    if (visited.has(id) || cyclic.has(id)) return;
    visited.add(id);
    for (const dependencyId of dependencies[id] ?? []) visit(dependencyId);
    order.push(id);
  }

  for (const definition of definitions.values()) visit(definition.region.id);
  return order;
}

function definitionsHasId(
  definitions: Map<string, Definition>,
  id: string,
): boolean {
  return [...definitions.values()].some(
    (definition) => definition.region.id === id,
  );
}

function error(
  code: CalculationErrorCode,
  message: string,
): RegionCalculationResult {
  return { status: "error", code, message };
}

function toResult(failure: unknown): RegionCalculationResult {
  if (failure instanceof CalculationFailure)
    return error(failure.code, failure.message);
  return error("invalid-expression", "Unable to evaluate this expression");
}


===== ./domain/units/quantities.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addQuantities,
  divideQuantities,
  multiplyQuantities,
  powerQuantity,
  quantity,
  subtractQuantities,
  valueInUnit,
} from "./quantities";
import { UnitError } from "./registry";

describe("SI quantity operations", () => {
  it("converts metric length, force, and pressure units", () => {
    expect(valueInUnit(quantity(25, "mm"), "m")).toBeCloseTo(0.025);
    expect(valueInUnit(quantity(3, "kN"), "N")).toBe(3000);
    expect(valueInUnit(quantity(2.5, "MPa"), "Pa")).toBe(2_500_000);
  });

  it("checks dimensions during conversion and addition", () => {
    expect(() => valueInUnit(quantity(1, "m"), "s")).toThrowError(UnitError);
    expect(() =>
      addQuantities(quantity(1, "m"), quantity(1, "s")),
    ).toThrowError(/incompatible dimensions/);
  });

  it("combines dimensions for engineering operations", () => {
    const torque = multiplyQuantities(quantity(120, "N"), quantity(0.35, "m"));
    expect(torque.magnitudeSI).toBe(42);
    const pressure = divideQuantities(
      quantity(10, "N"),
      powerQuantity(quantity(2, "m"), 2),
    );
    expect(pressure.dimension).toMatchObject({ length: -1, mass: 1, time: -2 });
  });

  it("distinguishes absolute and relative temperatures", () => {
    const difference = subtractQuantities(
      quantity(25, "degC"),
      quantity(20, "degC"),
    );
    expect(difference.temperatureKind).toBe("temperature-difference");
    expect(valueInUnit(difference, "delta_degC")).toBeCloseTo(5);
    expect(valueInUnit(quantity(0, "degC"), "K")).toBeCloseTo(273.15);
    expect(() =>
      addQuantities(quantity(20, "degC"), quantity(10, "degC")),
    ).toThrow(/cannot be added/);
  });
});


===== ./domain/units/quantities.ts =====
import { dimensionsEqual, type Dimension, type Quantity } from "./model";
import { getUnit, UnitError } from "./registry";

export function quantity(magnitude: number, unitSymbol: string): Quantity {
  if (!Number.isFinite(magnitude))
    throw new UnitError(
      "invalid-magnitude",
      "Quantity magnitude must be finite",
    );
  const unit = getUnit(unitSymbol);
  return {
    magnitudeSI: magnitude * unit.scaleToSI + unit.offsetToSI,
    dimension: unit.dimension,
    temperatureKind: unit.kind,
    displayUnit: unit.symbol,
  };
}

export function valueInUnit(value: Quantity, unitSymbol: string): number {
  const unit = getUnit(unitSymbol);
  requireCompatible(value, unit.dimension, unit.kind);
  return (value.magnitudeSI - unit.offsetToSI) / unit.scaleToSI;
}

export function addQuantities(left: Quantity, right: Quantity): Quantity {
  requireSameDimension(left, right);
  let kind = left.temperatureKind;
  if (isAbsolute(left) && isAbsolute(right))
    throw temperatureError("Two absolute temperatures cannot be added");
  if (
    (isAbsolute(left) && isDifference(right)) ||
    (isDifference(left) && isAbsolute(right))
  )
    kind = "absolute-temperature";
  else if (left.temperatureKind !== right.temperatureKind)
    throw temperatureError("Temperature kinds are incompatible");
  return {
    ...left,
    magnitudeSI: left.magnitudeSI + right.magnitudeSI,
    temperatureKind: kind,
  };
}

export function subtractQuantities(left: Quantity, right: Quantity): Quantity {
  requireSameDimension(left, right);
  let kind = left.temperatureKind;
  if (isAbsolute(left) && isAbsolute(right)) kind = "temperature-difference";
  else if (isAbsolute(left) && isDifference(right))
    kind = "absolute-temperature";
  else if (isDifference(left) && isAbsolute(right))
    throw temperatureError(
      "A temperature difference cannot subtract an absolute temperature",
    );
  else if (left.temperatureKind !== right.temperatureKind)
    throw temperatureError("Temperature kinds are incompatible");
  return {
    ...left,
    magnitudeSI: left.magnitudeSI - right.magnitudeSI,
    temperatureKind: kind,
  };
}

export function multiplyQuantities(left: Quantity, right: Quantity): Quantity {
  rejectAbsoluteTemperature(left, "multiply");
  rejectAbsoluteTemperature(right, "multiply");
  return {
    magnitudeSI: left.magnitudeSI * right.magnitudeSI,
    dimension: combineDimensions(left.dimension, right.dimension, 1),
    temperatureKind: "linear",
    displayUnit: "SI",
  };
}

export function divideQuantities(left: Quantity, right: Quantity): Quantity {
  rejectAbsoluteTemperature(left, "divide");
  rejectAbsoluteTemperature(right, "divide");
  if (right.magnitudeSI === 0)
    throw new UnitError("division-by-zero", "Division by zero");
  return {
    magnitudeSI: left.magnitudeSI / right.magnitudeSI,
    dimension: combineDimensions(left.dimension, right.dimension, -1),
    temperatureKind: "linear",
    displayUnit: "SI",
  };
}

export function powerQuantity(value: Quantity, exponent: number): Quantity {
  rejectAbsoluteTemperature(value, "raise to a power");
  if (!Number.isFinite(exponent))
    throw new UnitError("invalid-magnitude", "Exponent must be finite");
  if (!isDimensionless(value.dimension) && !Number.isInteger(exponent)) {
    throw new UnitError(
      "invalid-magnitude",
      "A dimensioned quantity requires an integer exponent",
    );
  }
  return {
    magnitudeSI: value.magnitudeSI ** exponent,
    dimension: scaleDimension(value.dimension, exponent),
    temperatureKind: "linear",
    displayUnit: "SI",
  };
}

function requireSameDimension(left: Quantity, right: Quantity): void {
  if (!dimensionsEqual(left.dimension, right.dimension)) {
    throw new UnitError(
      "dimension-mismatch",
      "Quantities have incompatible dimensions",
    );
  }
}

function requireCompatible(
  value: Quantity,
  dimension: Dimension,
  kind: Quantity["temperatureKind"],
): void {
  if (!dimensionsEqual(value.dimension, dimension))
    throw new UnitError(
      "dimension-mismatch",
      "Cannot convert between incompatible dimensions",
    );
  if (value.temperatureKind !== kind)
    throw temperatureError(
      "Absolute temperatures and temperature differences are not interchangeable",
    );
}

function combineDimensions(
  left: Dimension,
  right: Dimension,
  sign: 1 | -1,
): Dimension {
  return {
    length: left.length + sign * right.length,
    mass: left.mass + sign * right.mass,
    time: left.time + sign * right.time,
    electricCurrent: left.electricCurrent + sign * right.electricCurrent,
    temperature: left.temperature + sign * right.temperature,
    amount: left.amount + sign * right.amount,
    luminousIntensity: left.luminousIntensity + sign * right.luminousIntensity,
  };
}

function scaleDimension(value: Dimension, exponent: number): Dimension {
  return {
    length: value.length * exponent,
    mass: value.mass * exponent,
    time: value.time * exponent,
    electricCurrent: value.electricCurrent * exponent,
    temperature: value.temperature * exponent,
    amount: value.amount * exponent,
    luminousIntensity: value.luminousIntensity * exponent,
  };
}

function isDimensionless(value: Dimension): boolean {
  return dimensionsEqual(value, {
    length: 0,
    mass: 0,
    time: 0,
    electricCurrent: 0,
    temperature: 0,
    amount: 0,
    luminousIntensity: 0,
  });
}

function isAbsolute(value: Quantity): boolean {
  return value.temperatureKind === "absolute-temperature";
}
function isDifference(value: Quantity): boolean {
  return value.temperatureKind === "temperature-difference";
}

function rejectAbsoluteTemperature(value: Quantity, operation: string): void {
  if (isAbsolute(value))
    throw temperatureError(
      `Absolute temperatures cannot be used in ${operation}`,
    );
}

function temperatureError(message: string): UnitError {
  return new UnitError("temperature-kind-mismatch", message);
}


===== ./domain/units/registry.ts =====
import type { Dimension, UnitDefinition, UnitKind } from "./model";

const dimension = (values: Partial<Dimension> = {}): Dimension => ({
  length: values.length ?? 0,
  mass: values.mass ?? 0,
  time: values.time ?? 0,
  electricCurrent: values.electricCurrent ?? 0,
  temperature: values.temperature ?? 0,
  amount: values.amount ?? 0,
  luminousIntensity: values.luminousIntensity ?? 0,
});

const linear = (
  symbol: string,
  name: string,
  dimensions: Dimension,
  scaleToSI: number,
): UnitDefinition => ({
  symbol,
  name,
  dimension: dimensions,
  scaleToSI,
  offsetToSI: 0,
  kind: "linear",
});

const temperature = (
  symbol: string,
  name: string,
  scaleToSI: number,
  offsetToSI: number,
  kind: UnitKind,
): UnitDefinition => ({
  symbol,
  name,
  dimension: dimension({ temperature: 1 }),
  scaleToSI,
  offsetToSI,
  kind,
});

export const unitDefinitions: readonly UnitDefinition[] = [
  linear("m", "metre", dimension({ length: 1 }), 1),
  linear("mm", "millimetre", dimension({ length: 1 }), 1e-3),
  linear("cm", "centimetre", dimension({ length: 1 }), 1e-2),
  linear("km", "kilometre", dimension({ length: 1 }), 1e3),
  linear("kg", "kilogram", dimension({ mass: 1 }), 1),
  linear("g", "gram", dimension({ mass: 1 }), 1e-3),
  linear("s", "second", dimension({ time: 1 }), 1),
  linear("min", "minute", dimension({ time: 1 }), 60),
  linear("h", "hour", dimension({ time: 1 }), 3600),
  linear("rad", "radian", dimension(), 1),
  linear("deg", "degree", dimension(), Math.PI / 180),
  linear("N", "newton", dimension({ length: 1, mass: 1, time: -2 }), 1),
  linear("kN", "kilonewton", dimension({ length: 1, mass: 1, time: -2 }), 1e3),
  linear("Pa", "pascal", dimension({ length: -1, mass: 1, time: -2 }), 1),
  linear(
    "kPa",
    "kilopascal",
    dimension({ length: -1, mass: 1, time: -2 }),
    1e3,
  ),
  linear(
    "MPa",
    "megapascal",
    dimension({ length: -1, mass: 1, time: -2 }),
    1e6,
  ),
  linear(
    "GPa",
    "gigapascal",
    dimension({ length: -1, mass: 1, time: -2 }),
    1e9,
  ),
  linear("J", "joule", dimension({ length: 2, mass: 1, time: -2 }), 1),
  linear("W", "watt", dimension({ length: 2, mass: 1, time: -3 }), 1),
  temperature("K", "kelvin", 1, 0, "absolute-temperature"),
  temperature("degC", "degree Celsius", 1, 273.15, "absolute-temperature"),
  temperature(
    "delta_K",
    "kelvin temperature difference",
    1,
    0,
    "temperature-difference",
  ),
  temperature(
    "delta_degC",
    "Celsius temperature difference",
    1,
    0,
    "temperature-difference",
  ),
];

const registry = new Map(unitDefinitions.map((unit) => [unit.symbol, unit]));

export function getUnit(symbol: string): UnitDefinition {
  const unit = registry.get(symbol);
  if (!unit) throw new UnitError("unknown-unit", `Unknown unit "${symbol}"`);
  return unit;
}

export class UnitError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code:
      | "unknown-unit"
      | "dimension-mismatch"
      | "temperature-kind-mismatch"
      | "invalid-magnitude"
      | "division-by-zero",
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}


===== ./domain/units/model.ts =====
export type Dimension = Readonly<{
  length: number;
  mass: number;
  time: number;
  electricCurrent: number;
  temperature: number;
  amount: number;
  luminousIntensity: number;
}>;

export type UnitKind =
  "linear" | "absolute-temperature" | "temperature-difference";

export type UnitDefinition = {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  dimension: Dimension;
  scaleToSI: number;
  offsetToSI: number;
  kind: UnitKind;
};

export type Quantity = {
  magnitudeSI: number;
  dimension: Dimension;
  temperatureKind: UnitKind;
  displayUnit: string;
};

export const DIMENSIONLESS: Dimension = Object.freeze({
  length: 0,
  mass: 0,
  time: 0,
  electricCurrent: 0,
  temperature: 0,
  amount: 0,
  luminousIntensity: 0,
});

export function dimensionsEqual(left: Dimension, right: Dimension): boolean {
  return Object.keys(DIMENSIONLESS).every(
    (key) => left[key as keyof Dimension] === right[key as keyof Dimension],
  );
}


===== ./domain/worksheet/serialization.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { MathExpression } from "../expressions/ast";
import { parseMathStatement } from "../expressions/parser";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "./model";
import { deserializeWorksheet, serializeWorksheet } from "./serialization";

const worksheet: WorksheetDocument = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "test-document",
  title: "Shaft calculation",
  settings: {
    page: { width: 210, height: 297, unit: "mm" },
    grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 },
  },
  regions: [
    {
      id: "label",
      type: "text",
      content: "Torque",
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 29.7 },
      zIndex: 0,
    },
    {
      id: "torque",
      type: "math",
      content: parseMathStatement("load * arm"),
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 92.07 },
      zIndex: 1,
    },
  ],
};

describe("worksheet serialization", () => {
  it("round-trips the validated structured document", () => {
    expect(deserializeWorksheet(serializeWorksheet(worksheet))).toEqual(
      worksheet,
    );
  });

  it("migrates the prototype document and percentage coordinates", () => {
    const migrated = deserializeWorksheet(
      JSON.stringify({
        title: "Legacy sheet",
        regions: [
          { id: "load", type: "math", content: "load := 142.35", x: 7, y: 21 },
          { id: "note", type: "text", content: "Torque", x: 7, y: 38 },
        ],
      }),
    );

    expect(migrated.schemaVersion).toBe(1);
    expect(migrated.regions[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "load",
      type: "math",
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 62.37 },
      content: {
        kind: "assignment",
        name: "load",
        expression: { kind: "number", value: 142.35 },
      },
    });
    expect(migrated.regions[1]).toMatchObject({
      position: { x: 14.7, y: 112.86 },
    });
  });

  it("rejects malformed, oversized, and deeply nested documents", () => {
    expect(() =>
      deserializeWorksheet(JSON.stringify({ schemaVersion: 1, regions: [] })),
    ).toThrow();
    expect(() => deserializeWorksheet(" ".repeat(2_000_001))).toThrow("2 MB");

    let expression: MathExpression = { kind: "number", value: 1 };
    for (let depth = 0; depth < 102; depth += 1) {
      expression = { kind: "unary", operator: "negative", operand: expression };
    }
    expect(() =>
      deserializeWorksheet(
        JSON.stringify({
          ...worksheet,
          regions: [
            {
              ...worksheet.regions[1],
              content: { kind: "expression", expression },
            },
          ],
        }),
      ),
    ).toThrow("nesting");
  });

  it("rejects duplicate region IDs and coordinates outside the configured page", () => {
    const duplicate = {
      ...worksheet,
      regions: [worksheet.regions[0], { ...worksheet.regions[0], zIndex: 2 }],
    };
    const outOfBounds = {
      ...worksheet,
      regions: [{ ...worksheet.regions[0], position: { x: 211, y: 0 } }],
    };
    expect(() => deserializeWorksheet(JSON.stringify(duplicate))).toThrow(
      "Duplicate region ID",
    );
    expect(() => deserializeWorksheet(JSON.stringify(outOfBounds))).toThrow(
      "fit within the worksheet page",
    );
  });

  it("rejects quantity AST nodes with unknown units", () => {
    const invalidQuantity = {
      ...worksheet,
      regions: [
        {
          ...worksheet.regions[1],
          content: {
            kind: "expression",
            expression: {
              kind: "quantity",
              magnitude: { kind: "number", value: 1 },
              unit: "lightyear",
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    };
    expect(() => deserializeWorksheet(JSON.stringify(invalidQuantity))).toThrow(
      /Unknown unit/,
    );
  });
});


===== ./domain/worksheet/validation.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";
import type { MathExpression } from "../expressions/ast";
import { getUnit } from "../units/registry";
import type { WorksheetDocument } from "./model";

const identifierSchema = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*$/)
  .max(128);

export const mathExpressionSchema: z.ZodType<MathExpression> = z.lazy(() =>
  z.union([
    z.object({ kind: z.literal("empty") }).strict(),
    z
      .object({ kind: z.literal("number"), value: z.number().finite() })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({ kind: z.literal("identifier"), name: identifierSchema })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({ kind: z.literal("constant"), name: z.enum(["pi", "e"]) })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("quantity"),
        magnitude: mathExpressionSchema,
        unit: z.string().min(1).max(32),
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("unary"),
        operator: z.enum(["positive", "negative"]),
        operand: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("binary"),
        operator: z.enum(["add", "subtract", "multiply", "divide", "power"]),
        left: mathExpressionSchema,
        right: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("function"),
        name: z.enum(["abs", "cos", "exp", "ln", "log", "sin", "sqrt", "tan"]),
        argument: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("fraction"),
        numerator: mathExpressionSchema,
        denominator: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({ kind: z.literal("root"), radicand: mathExpressionSchema })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("power"),
        base: mathExpressionSchema,
        exponent: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
    z
      .object({
        kind: z.literal("subscript"),
        base: mathExpressionSchema,
        subscript: mathExpressionSchema,
      })
      .strict(),
  ]),
) as z.ZodType<MathExpression>;

const mathStatementSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  z
    .object({ kind: z.literal("expression"), expression: mathExpressionSchema })
    .strict(),
  z
    .object({
      kind: z.literal("assignment"),
      name: identifierSchema,
      expression: mathExpressionSchema,
    })
    .strict(),
]);

const baseRegionSchema = z
  .object({
    id: z.string().min(1).max(128),
    position: z
      .object({
        x: z.number().finite().min(0).max(2100),
        y: z.number().finite().min(0).max(2970),
      })
      .strict(),
    size: z
      .object({
        width: z.number().positive().max(2100),
        height: z.number().positive().max(2970),
      })
      .strict()
      .optional(),
    zIndex: z.number().int().min(0).max(10000),
  })
  .strict();

export const worksheetDocumentSchema: z.ZodType<WorksheetDocument> = z
  .object({
    schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
    id: z.string().min(1).max(128),
    title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(160),
    settings: z
      .object({
        page: z
          .object({
            width: z.number().positive().max(2000),
            height: z.number().positive().max(3000),
            unit: z.literal("mm"),
          })
          .strict(),
        grid: z
          .object({
            visible: z.boolean(),
            spacing: z.number().positive().max(100),
          })
          .strict(),
      })
      .strict(),
    regions: z
      .array(
        z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
          baseRegionSchema.extend({
            type: z.literal("math"),
            content: mathStatementSchema,
          }),
          baseRegionSchema.extend({
            type: z.literal("text"),
            content: z.string().max(20000),
            presentation: z
              .object({ role: z.enum(["body", "heading"]) })
              .strict()
              .optional(),
          }),
        ]),
      )
      .max(2000),
  })
  .strict() as z.ZodType<WorksheetDocument>;

export function validateWorksheetDocument(value: unknown): WorksheetDocument {
  const document = worksheetDocumentSchema.parse(value);
  const regionIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const region of document.regions) {
    if (regionIds.has(region.id)) {
      throw new z.ZodError([
        {
          code: "custom",
          path: ["regions", region.id],
          message: `Duplicate region ID "${region.id}"`,
        },
      ]);
    }
    regionIds.add(region.id);
    if (
      region.position.x > document.settings.page.width ||
      region.position.y > document.settings.page.height ||
      (region.size &&
        (region.position.x + region.size.width > document.settings.page.width ||
          region.position.y + region.size.height >
            document.settings.page.height))
    ) {
      throw new z.ZodError([
        {
          code: "custom",
          path: ["regions", region.id, "position"],
          message:
            "Region position and size must fit within the worksheet page",
        },
      ]);
    }
    if (region.type === "math")
      assertExpressionDepth(region.content.expression, 0);
  }
  return document;
}

function assertExpressionDepth(
  expression: MathExpression,
  depth: number,
): void {
  if (depth > 100)
    throw new z.ZodError([
      {
        code: "custom",
        path: ["regions", "content"],
        message: "Expression nesting exceeds 100 levels",
      },
    ]);
  switch (expression.kind) {
    case "unary":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.operand, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "binary":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.left, depth + 1);
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.right, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "function":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.argument, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "fraction":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.numerator, depth + 1);
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.denominator, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "root":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.radicand, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "power":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.base, depth + 1);
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.exponent, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "subscript":
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.base, depth + 1);
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.subscript, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "quantity":
      try {
        getUnit(expression.unit);
      } catch {
        throw new z.ZodError([
          {
            code: "custom",
            path: ["regions", "content", "unit"],
            message: `Unknown unit "${expression.unit}"`,
          },
        ]);
      }
      assertExpressionDepth(expression.magnitude, depth + 1);
      break;
    case "empty":
    case "number":
    case "identifier":
    case "constant":
      break;
  }
}


===== ./domain/worksheet/serialization.ts =====
import { parseMathStatement } from "../expressions/parser";
import {
  DEFAULT_PAGE,
  type WorksheetDocument,
  type WorksheetRegion,
} from "./model";
import { validateWorksheetDocument } from "./validation";

const MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES = 2_000_000;

export function serializeWorksheet(document: WorksheetDocument): string {
  return JSON.stringify(validateWorksheetDocument(document));
}

export function deserializeWorksheet(serialized: string): WorksheetDocument {
  if (new TextEncoder().encode(serialized).byteLength > MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES) {
    throw new RangeError("Worksheet document exceeds the 2 MB limit");
  }
  const value: unknown = JSON.parse(serialized);
  if (isCurrentDocument(value)) return validateWorksheetDocument(value);
  return validateWorksheetDocument(migrateLegacyDocument(value));
}

function isCurrentDocument(value: unknown): value is WorksheetDocument {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    "schemaVersion" in value &&
    value.schemaVersion === 1
  );
}

function migrateLegacyDocument(value: unknown): WorksheetDocument {
  if (!isRecord(value) || !Array.isArray(value.regions))
    throw new TypeError("Unsupported worksheet document format");
  const regions: WorksheetRegion[] = [];
  value.regions.forEach((item, index) => {
    if (
      !isRecord(item) ||
      typeof item.id !== "string" ||
      typeof item.content !== "string"
    )
      return [];
    const position = {
      x: legacyCoordinate(item.x, DEFAULT_PAGE.width),
      y: legacyCoordinate(item.y, DEFAULT_PAGE.height),
    };
    if (item.type === "text") {
      regions.push({
        id: item.id,
        type: "text",
        content: item.content,
        position,
        zIndex: index,
      });
      return;
    }
    if (item.type === "math") {
      regions.push({
        id: item.id,
        type: "math",
        content: parseMathStatement(item.content),
        position,
        zIndex: index,
      });
    }
  });

  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    id: typeof value.id === "string" ? value.id : "worksheet-local",
    title:
      typeof value.title === "string" && value.title.trim()
        ? value.title
        : "Untitled worksheet",
    settings: { page: DEFAULT_PAGE, grid: { visible: true, spacing: 4.25 } },
    regions,
  };
}

function legacyCoordinate(value: unknown, dimension: number): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) return 7;
  return Number(
    Math.min(dimension, Math.max(0, (value / 100) * dimension)).toFixed(6),
  );
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}


===== ./domain/worksheet/model.ts =====
import type { MathStatement } from "../expressions/ast";

export type Point = { x: number; y: number };
export type RegionSize = { width: number; height: number };

export type RegionBase = {
  id: string;
  position: Point;
  size?: RegionSize;
  zIndex: number;
};

export type MathRegion = RegionBase & {
  type: "math";
  content: MathStatement;
};

export type TextRegion = RegionBase & {
  type: "text";
  content: string;
  presentation?: { role?: "body" | "heading" };
};

export type WorksheetRegion = MathRegion | TextRegion;

export type WorksheetDocument = {
  schemaVersion: 1;
  id: string;
  title: string;
  settings: {
    page: { width: number; height: number; unit: "mm" };
    grid: { visible: boolean; spacing: number };
  };
  regions: WorksheetRegion[];
};

export const DEFAULT_PAGE = { width: 210, height: 297, unit: "mm" as const };
